## Day 11: Present Simple Negative (Don't / Doesn't & Base Verb Mechanics) {#day-11-day_11}
**शीर्षक**: वर्तमान काल के नकारात्मक वाक्य (Don't / Doesn't और मुख्य क्रिया का मूल रूप)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.1` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 05 (Present BE), Day 08–09 (Subject Pronouns & Present Affirmative)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Form accurate negative sentences in Present Simple using 'do not / don't' with plural subjects and 'I/you'.
  - **HI**: I, You, We, They के साथ 'don't + Base Verb' का सही प्रयोग करना सीखना।
- **EN**: Form accurate negative sentences in Present Simple using 'does not / doesn't' with third-person singular subjects (he, she, it).
  - **HI**: He, She, It और एकवचन संज्ञाओं के साथ 'doesn't + Base Verb' का प्रयोग करना।
- **EN**: Master the mandatory base verb rule: always revert the main verb to its base form after doesn't (e.g., 'He doesn't work', NEVER 'He doesn't works').
  - **HI**: यह पक्का करना कि 'doesn't' के बाद मुख्य क्रिया में से 's/es' हमेशा हट जाता है (e.g. He doesn't work)।
- **EN**: Eliminate direct Hindi negative transfer errors like '*I not know*' or '*He don't likes*'.
  - **HI**: हिन्दी के 'मैं नहीं जानता' का गलत अनुवाद '*I not know*' या '*He don't*' करने से बचना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
हिन्दी में जब हम किसी काम को वर्तमान में 'नहीं' करते हैं, तो हम केवल क्रिया के आगे 'नहीं' लगा देते हैं (जैसे: 'मैं चाय नहीं पीता', 'वह यहाँ नहीं रहता')। परन्तु अंग्रेज़ी में साधारण क्रियाओं (Action Verbs) को नकारात्मक बनाने के लिए सहायक क्रिया 'Do' या 'Does' के साथ 'Not' जोड़ना अनिवार्य होता है। जब कर्ता I, You, We, They या बहुवचन हो, तो 'don't + क्रिया का मूल रूप' (Base Verb) आता है। जब कर्ता He, She, It या एकवचन नाम हो, तो 'doesn't + क्रिया का मूल रूप' आता है। सबसे महत्वपूर्ण नियम: 'doesn't' आने पर मुख्य क्रिया से 's' या 'es' हट जाता है!

**English Explanation**:
In English Present Simple, dynamic action verbs cannot be made negative by simply adding 'not' (saying '*I not go*' is ungrammatical). We must use the auxiliary operator DO/DOES + NOT. For plural subjects and I/You, we use 'don't + base verb'. For third-person singular subjects (he/she/it), we use 'doesn't + base verb'. Crucially, the auxiliary 'does' absorbs the third-person marker, so the main verb must strictly return to its bare infinitive form.

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Subject-Auxiliary Agreement (don't vs. doesn't)** [Universal Grammar Standard]
  Use 'don't' with {I, You, We, They, Plural Nouns}. Use 'doesn't' with {He, She, It, Singular Nouns / Names}.
- **Rule 2: The Base Verb Principle after 'Doesn't'** [Core Syntax]
  Because 'does' already carries the third-person '-es' inflection, the main verb MUST be in base form (V1). Correct: 'She doesn't drive.' Incorrect: '*She doesn't drives.*'
- **Rule 3: Negative Possession with Action/Dynamic Verb 'Have'** [Standard Modern English]
  In modern English, negative possession uses 'don't have' or 'doesn't have'. Correct: 'He doesn't have a car.' Never say: '*He doesn't has a car.*'
- **Rule 4: BE Verb (Stative) vs. Action Verb (Dynamic) Negation** [Contrastive Structural Rule]
  Do NOT mix 'am not / is not' with action verbs. Say 'I am not ready' (stative) vs. 'I don't work today' (action). Never say '*I am not work*'.

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "subjectGroup": "I / You / We / They / Plural",
    "formula": "Subject + don't (do not) + Base Verb (V1) + [Object/Complement]",
    "exampleEn": "They don't live in Delhi.",
    "meaningHi": "वे दिल्ली में नहीं रहते हैं।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "He / She / It / Singular Name",
    "formula": "Subject + doesn't (does not) + Base Verb (V1) + [Object/Complement]",
    "exampleEn": "Rohit doesn't drink coffee.",
    "meaningHi": "रोहित कॉफ़ी नहीं पीता है।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Third-Person Possession",
    "formula": "Subject + doesn't have + [Noun Object]",
    "exampleEn": "She doesn't have a laptop.",
    "meaningHi": "उसके पास लैपटॉप नहीं है।"
  },
  {
    "subjectGroup": "Stative BE Contrast",
    "formula": "Subject + is/am/are + not + [Adjective/Noun]",
    "exampleEn": "He is not angry.",
    "meaningHi": "वह नाराज़ नहीं है।"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Double-Marking '-s' with 'doesn't' and omitting auxiliary ('I not know')
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'वह काम नहीं करता' में 'करता' नहीं बदलता, इसलिए कई भारतीय शिक्षार्थी 'He doesn't works' या 'He not work' बोल देते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*She doesn't likes tea*' or '*I not understand*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: In English, a sentence can only mark the third-person singular once per clause. 'Does' takes the marker, freeing the main verb. Furthermore, bare 'not' cannot negate a finite lexical verb without an auxiliary.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Always say 'She doesn't like tea' and 'I don't understand'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day11_vocab_001` | **Understand** | Verb | `/ˌʌn.dəˈstænd/` | समझना | I don't understand this question. <br> *मैं इस सवाल को नहीं समझता।* |
| `day11_vocab_002` | **Remember** | Verb | `/rɪˈmem.bər/` | याद रखना / स्मरण करना | He doesn't remember my phone number. <br> *उसे मेरा फ़ोन नंबर याद नहीं रहता।* |
| `day11_vocab_003` | **Agree** | Verb | `/əˈɡriː/` | सहमत होना | We don't agree with this decision. <br> *हम इस फ़ैसले से सहमत नहीं हैं।* |
| `day11_vocab_004` | **Need** | Verb | `/niːd/` | ज़रूरत होना / आवश्यकता होना | She doesn't need any help right now. <br> *उसे अभी किसी मदद की ज़रूरत नहीं है।* |
| `day11_vocab_005` | **Believe** | Verb | `/bɪˈliːv/` | विश्वास करना / मानना | I don't believe false rumors. <br> *मैं झूठी अफ़वाहों पर विश्वास नहीं करता।* |
| `day11_vocab_006` | **Drive** | Verb | `/draɪv/` | गाड़ी चलाना | Rohan doesn't drive at night. <br> *रोहन रात में गाड़ी नहीं चलाता है।* |
| `day11_vocab_007` | **Complain** | Verb | `/kəmˈpleɪn/` | शिकायत करना | They don't complain about the food. <br> *वे खाने की शिकायत नहीं करते हैं।* |
| `day11_vocab_008` | **Forget** | Verb | `/fəˈɡet/` | भूलना | My mother doesn't forget important dates. <br> *मेरी माँ महत्वपूर्ण तारीखें नहीं भूलती हैं।* |
| `day11_vocab_009` | **Smoke** | Verb | `/sməʊk/` | धूम्रपान करना | He doesn't smoke cigarettes. <br> *वह सिगरेट नहीं पीता है।* |
| `day11_vocab_010` | **Cost** | Verb | `/kɒst/` | कीमत होना / लागत होना | This ticket doesn't cost much money. <br> *इस टिकट की कीमत ज़्यादा नहीं है।* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day11_phrase_001` [Neutral / Conversational]: **"I don't think so."** — *मुझे ऐसा नहीं लगता।*
- `day11_phrase_002` [Neutral]: **"It doesn't matter at all."** — *इससे कोई फ़र्क नहीं पड़ता।*
- `day11_phrase_003` [Formal / Neutral]: **"He doesn't have time today."** — *उसके पास आज समय नहीं है।*
- `day11_phrase_004` [Polite / Social]: **"We don't mind waiting."** — *हमें इंतज़ार करने में कोई परेशानी नहीं है।*
- `day11_phrase_005` [Informal]: **"I don't have any clue."** — *मुझे ज़रा भी अंदाज़ा नहीं है।*
- `day11_phrase_006` [Neutral]: **"She doesn't speak French."** — *वह फ़्रेंच नहीं बोलती है।*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day11_example_001`: **I don't drink tea in the evening.**  
  *मैं शाम को चाय नहीं पीता हूँ।*
- `day11_example_002`: **Aman doesn't work on Saturdays.**  
  *अमन शनिवार को काम नहीं करता है।*
- `day11_example_003`: **We don't live in a big city.**  
  *हम किसी बड़े शहर में नहीं रहते हैं।*
- `day11_example_004`: **She doesn't have a passport yet.**  
  *उसके पास अभी पासपोर्ट नहीं है।*
- `day11_example_005`: **My parents don't watch television late at night.**  
  *मेरे माता-पिता देर रात टीवी नहीं देखते हैं।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Contracted /doʊnt/ with silent or glottal 't', Two syllables in /ˈdʌz.ənt/ with weak schwa
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Avoid separating 'do not' robotically in casual talk unless emphasizing. In connected speech, 'I don't know' softens to /aɪ dəˈnoʊ/.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day11_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "I don't know the exact answer."
- `day11_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "She doesn't live here anymore."
- `day11_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "It doesn't make any difference."
- `day11_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "We don't have enough time."

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day11_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `NEGATION_RULES`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to say 'वह मांस नहीं खाता है':
**Options**: `He doesn't eats meat.` | `He don't eat meat.` | `He doesn't eat meat.` | `He not eat meat.`
- **Correct Answer**: `He doesn't eat meat.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'He' requires 'doesn't' followed by the base verb 'eat' without '-s'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'He' के साथ 'doesn't' लगता है और मुख्य क्रिया 'eat' में 's' नहीं लगता।

#### 2. `[day11_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `NEGATION_RULES`)
**Prompt**: Select the correct negative form for 'I work on Sundays':
**Options**: `I doesn't work on Sundays.` | `I not work on Sundays.` | `I don't work on Sundays.` | `I am not work on Sundays.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I don't work on Sundays.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'I' takes the auxiliary 'don't' with dynamic verbs.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'I' के साथ नकारात्मक में 'don't' का प्रयोग होता है।

#### 3. `[day11_ex_003]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `POSSESSION_NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: Which sentence is grammatically CORRECT for negative possession?
**Options**: `Pooja doesn't has a car.` | `Pooja doesn't have a car.` | `Pooja don't have a car.` | `Pooja not has a car.`
- **Correct Answer**: `Pooja doesn't have a car.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: After 'doesn't', the verb 'have' must remain in its base form 'have', never 'has'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Doesn't' के बाद हमेशा 'have' आता है, 'has' कभी नहीं।

#### 4. `[day11_ex_004]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PRONOUN_AGREEMENT`)
**Prompt**: Identify the correct sentence: 'वे अंग्रेज़ी नहीं बोलते हैं।'
**Options**: `They doesn't speak English.` | `They don't speaks English.` | `They don't speak English.` | `They aren't speak English.`
- **Correct Answer**: `They don't speak English.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'They' pairs with 'don't' and the base verb 'speak'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'They' बहुवचन है, इसलिए 'don't speak' सही है।

#### 5. `[day11_ex_005]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `SUBJECT_VERB_AGREEMENT`)
**Prompt**: Complete the sentence: 'My brother ______ like spicy food.'
**Options**: `don't` | `doesn't` | `is not` | `not`
- **Correct Answer**: `doesn't`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'My brother' is a singular third-person subject, requiring 'doesn't'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'My brother' एकवचन है, इसलिए 'doesn't' आएगा।

#### 6. `[day11_ex_006]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: I ______ (not / know) the way to the train station.
- **Correct Answer**: `don't know`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `do not know`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'I' takes 'don't know' in Present Simple negative.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'I' के साथ 'don't know' का प्रयोग करें।

#### 7. `[day11_ex_007]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: Sneha ______ (not / understand) the instructions.
- **Correct Answer**: `doesn't understand`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `does not understand`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Sneha' is singular, so use 'doesn't' + base verb 'understand'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: एकवचन नाम के साथ 'doesn't understand' आएगा।

#### 8. `[day11_ex_008]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: We ______ (not / agree) with your proposal.
- **Correct Answer**: `don't agree`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `do not agree`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'We' takes 'don't agree'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'We' के साथ 'don't agree' आएगा।

#### 9. `[day11_ex_009]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: This phone ______ (not / cost) a lot of money.
- **Correct Answer**: `doesn't cost`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `does not cost`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'This phone' is singular (it), so use 'doesn't cost'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'This phone' (It) के लिए 'doesn't cost' सही रूप है।

#### 10. `[day11_ex_010]` [FILL_BLANK] (medium | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: The trains ______ (not / stop) at this small station.
- **Correct Answer**: `don't stop`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `do not stop`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'The trains' is plural, requiring 'don't stop'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'The trains' बहुवचन है, इसलिए 'don't stop' आएगा।

#### 11. `[day11_ex_011]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'He doesn't lives in Mumbai.'
- **Correct Answer**: `He doesn't live in Mumbai.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Remove '-s' from 'lives' after 'doesn't'. Use base form 'live'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Doesn't' आने पर 'lives' का 's' हटाकर मूल क्रिया 'live' लिखें।

#### 12. `[day11_ex_012]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'I not understand this lesson.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I don't understand this lesson.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `I do not understand this lesson.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Present Simple requires auxiliary 'don't' before 'understand'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: अंग्रेज़ी में सिर्फ 'not understand' नहीं बोलते, 'don't understand' बोलना आवश्यक है।

#### 13. `[day11_ex_013]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'She doesn't has any brother.'
- **Correct Answer**: `She doesn't have any brother.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `She doesn't have any brothers.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: After 'doesn't', always use 'have', never 'has'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Doesn't' के बाद हमेशा 'have' आता है।

#### 14. `[day11_ex_014]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'My father don't drink coffee.'
- **Correct Answer**: `My father doesn't drink coffee.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `My father does not drink coffee.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'My father' is singular, so it takes 'doesn't', not 'don't'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'My father' एकवचन है, इसलिए 'don't' की जगह 'doesn't' आएगा।

#### 15. `[day11_ex_015]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'I am not work in a bank.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I don't work in a bank.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `I do not work in a bank.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Do not use 'am not' with action verbs in Present Simple. Use 'don't work'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सामान्य क्रियाओं में 'am not work' नहीं, 'don't work' कहा जाता है।

#### 16. `[day11_ex_016]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a grammatically correct negative sentence:
- **Correct Answer**: `Suresh doesn't like black tea`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject (Suresh) + Auxiliary (doesn't) + Verb (like) + Object (black tea).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: Suresh doesn't like black tea.

#### 17. `[day11_ex_017]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a correct sentence:
- **Correct Answer**: `We don't work on Sundays`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject (We) + don't + Base Verb (work) + Time Prepositional Phrase (on Sundays).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: We don't work on Sundays.

#### 18. `[day11_ex_018]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a correct sentence:
- **Correct Answer**: `He doesn't remember my name`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject (He) + doesn't + Verb (remember) + Object (my name).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: He doesn't remember my name.

#### 19. `[day11_ex_019]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a correct sentence:
- **Correct Answer**: `They don't have enough money`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject (They) + don't + have + Object (enough money).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: They don't have enough money.

#### 20. `[day11_ex_020]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a correct sentence:
- **Correct Answer**: `It doesn't matter at all`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject (It) + doesn't + Verb (matter) + Adverbial phrase (at all).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: It doesn't matter at all.

#### 21. `[day11_ex_021]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'मैं उस व्यक्ति को नहीं जानता हूँ।'
- **Correct Answer**: `I don't know that person.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `I do not know that person.`, `I don't know that man.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'I don't know' is the natural Present Simple negative.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'I don't know that person.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 22. `[day11_ex_022]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'वह कार नहीं चलाती है।'
- **Correct Answer**: `She doesn't drive a car.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `She does not drive a car.`, `She doesn't drive cars.`, `She doesn't drive.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'She doesn't drive a car' uses third-person singular negation.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'She doesn't drive a car.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 23. `[day11_ex_023]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (medium | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'हम देर से नहीं सोते हैं।'
- **Correct Answer**: `We don't sleep late.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `We do not sleep late.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'We don't sleep late' expresses habitual negation.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'We don't sleep late.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 24. `[day11_ex_024]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'रोहन यहाँ काम नहीं करता है।'
- **Correct Answer**: `Rohan doesn't work here.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Rohan does not work here.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Singular proper noun takes 'doesn't work here'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Rohan doesn't work here.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 25. `[day11_ex_025]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'मेरे पास पर्याप्त समय नहीं है।'
- **Correct Answer**: `I don't have enough time.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `I do not have enough time.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'I don't have enough time' is the standard negative possession structure.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'I don't have enough time.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 26. `[day11_ex_026]` [TRANSLATION_EN_HI] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into natural Hindi: 'They don't live in this neighborhood.'
- **Correct Answer**: `वे इस पड़ोस में नहीं रहते हैं।`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `वे इस इलाके में नहीं रहते हैं।`, `वे इस मोहल्ले में नहीं रहते हैं।`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'They don't live' translates to 'वे नहीं रहते हैं'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'They don't live in this neighborhood' का अर्थ है 'वे इस पड़ोस/मोहल्ले में नहीं रहते हैं।'

#### 27. `[day11_ex_027]` [TRANSLATION_EN_HI] (medium | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into natural Hindi: 'She doesn't believe your story.'
- **Correct Answer**: `वह आपकी कहानी पर विश्वास नहीं करती है।`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `वह तुम्हारी बात पर यकीन नहीं करती है।`, `वह आपकी बात पर विश्वास नहीं करती है।`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'She doesn't believe' translates to 'वह विश्वास नहीं करती है'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'वह आपकी कहानी/बात पर विश्वास नहीं करती है।' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 28. `[day11_ex_028]` [LISTENING_DICTATION] (medium | `LISTENING`)
**Prompt**: Listen to the audio and type exactly what you hear:
- **Correct Answer**: `My parents don't watch television in the afternoon.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `My parents do not watch television in the afternoon.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Notice the plural subject 'My parents' taking 'don't watch'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: बहुवचन कर्ता 'My parents' के साथ 'don't watch' आया है।

#### 29. `[day11_ex_029]` [LISTENING_DICTATION] (easy | `LISTENING`)
**Prompt**: Listen to the audio and write down the sentence:
- **Correct Answer**: `He doesn't smoke cigarettes.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `He does not smoke cigarettes.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Listen for the contraction 'doesn't' followed by 'smoke'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'He doesn't smoke cigarettes.' ध्यान से सुनकर लिखें।

#### 30. `[day11_ex_030]` [MATCHING] (easy | `MATCHING`)
**Prompt**: Match the subjects with their correct negative auxiliary verbs:
- **Correct Answer**: `Matched correctly`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Singular nouns and third-person singular pronouns take 'doesn't'; plural nouns and you take 'don't'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: एकवचन के साथ 'doesn't' और बहुवचन/You के साथ 'don't' का मिलान करें।

#### 31. `[day11_ex_031]` [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (easy | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the response: Speaker A: 'Do you want another cup of tea?' Speaker B: 'No, thank you. I ______ (not / drink) tea after 6 PM.'
- **Correct Answer**: `don't drink`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `do not drink`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Speaker B is talking about their own habit: 'I don't drink'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: खुद के लिए 'I don't drink' आएगा।

#### 32. `[day11_ex_032]` [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (easy | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the conversation: Speaker A: 'Why isn't Vikram driving today?' Speaker B: 'He ______ (not / have) his driving license with him.'
- **Correct Answer**: `doesn't have`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `does not have`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Vikram is a third-person singular subject: 'He doesn't have'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'He' के साथ 'doesn't have' आएगा।

#### 33. `[day11_ex_033]` [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (easy | `SPEAKING`)
**Prompt**: Speak your answer aloud: State two foods or drinks that you do NOT like using 'I don't like...'.
- **Correct Answer**: `I don't like [Food 1] and I don't like [Food 2].`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'I don't like...' followed by the food item.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'I don't like...' बोलकर दो खाद्य पदार्थों के नाम बताएं।

#### 34. `[day11_ex_034]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: What is the negative form of 'He works hard'?
- **Correct Answer**: `He doesn't work hard.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `He does not work hard.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Invert 'works' to 'doesn't work'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'He works hard' का नकारात्मक 'He doesn't work hard' होता है।

#### 35. `[day11_ex_035]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: What is the negative form of 'They have a pet dog'?
- **Correct Answer**: `They don't have a pet dog.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `They do not have a pet dog.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Negate with 'They don't have a pet dog'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'They have a pet dog' का नकारात्मक 'They don't have a pet dog' होता है।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Discussing daily habits, dietary preferences, and office routines with a new co-worker during lunch.

#### Dialogue (`day11_dialogue_001`):
- **Kavita**: "Would you like to try some of my curry, David?"  
  *(डेविड, क्या आप मेरी थोड़ी करी चखना चाहेंगे?)*
- **David**: "Thank you, Kavita, but I don't eat spicy food. My stomach doesn't handle chilies well."  
  *(धन्यवाद कविता, लेकिन मैं तीखा खाना नहीं खाता। मेरा पेट मिर्च बर्दाश्त नहीं कर पाता।)*
- **Kavita**: "Oh, I see! What about dessert? Do you eat sweets?"  
  *(अरे, समझी! मीठे के बारे में क्या ख्याल है? क्या आप मिठाई खाते हैं?)*
- **David**: "Yes, I love dessert! But my colleague Rahul doesn't like sugar at all."  
  *(हाँ, मुझे मीठा बहुत पसंद है! लेकिन मेरे सहकर्मी राहुल को चीनी बिल्कुल पसंद नहीं है।)*
- **Kavita**: "That's fine. We don't force anyone. Let's order some mild fruit salad."  
  *(कोई बात नहीं। हम किसी पर दबाव नहीं डालते। चलिए हल्का फ्रूट सलाद मंगवाते हैं।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Record 3 negative sentences about your daily work or study habits: one using 'I don't...', one about a friend using 'He/She doesn't...', and one using 'We don't...'.
- **HI**: अपनी दिनचर्या के बारे में 3 नकारात्मक वाक्य बोलें: एक 'I don't...', एक 'He/She doesn't...', और एक 'We don't...'।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day11_assess_001]` **Why is 'She doesn't works here' grammatically incorrect?** ➔ *Because 'doesn't' already contains the third-person '-es' inflection; the main verb must remain in its base form 'work'.* [Skill: Syntax Precision]
- `[day11_assess_002]` **What is the difference between 'I am not ready' and 'I don't study'?** ➔ *'Am not' negates a state of being (BE verb), while 'don't' negates a dynamic action verb (Present Simple).* [Skill: Grammar Discrimination]
- `[day11_assess_003]` **How do you negate third-person possession: 'He has a laptop'?** ➔ *He doesn't have a laptop (never '*doesn't has*').* [Skill: Possession Accuracy]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `PRESENT_SIMPLE_NEGATION_L1`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 12: Present Simple Questions & Short Answers (Do / Does & WH- Inversion) {#day-12-day_12}
**शीर्षक**: वर्तमान काल में प्रश्न और उत्तर (Do / Does तथा WH- प्रश्न रचना)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.1` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 08 (Present Simple Affirmative), Day 11 (Present Simple Negative)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Construct accurate Yes/No questions using initial auxiliary inversion (Do / Does + Subject + Base Verb).
  - **HI**: Yes/No प्रश्नों में कर्ता से पहले 'Do / Does' लगाकर सही प्रश्न बनाना सीखना।
- **EN**: Provide natural, native-like short answers without echoing full verbs (e.g., 'Yes, I do' / 'No, she doesn't').
  - **HI**: प्राकृतिक संक्षिप्त उत्तर देना सीखना (जैसे: 'Yes, I do' / 'No, she doesn't')।
- **EN**: Form open WH- information questions using the formula: WH- Word + do/does + Subject + Base Verb.
  - **HI**: WH- शब्द (What, Where, When, Why, How) के साथ 'WH + do/does + Subject + Base Verb' का नियम अपनाना।
- **EN**: Correct common Indian English transfer habits such as '*Where you work?*' or answering with '*Yes, I am*'.
  - **HI**: हिन्दी के प्रभाव से होने वाली गलतियाँ जैसे '*Where you live?*' या '*Yes, I am*' को पूरी तरह दूर करना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
हिन्दी में प्रश्न पूछने के लिए हम वाक्य के शुरू में 'क्या' लगा देते हैं (जैसे: 'क्या आप चाय पीते हैं?'), या बीच में प्रश्नवाचक शब्द डाल देते हैं ('आप कहाँ रहते हैं?')। लेकिन अंग्रेज़ी में सामान्य क्रियाओं के लिए प्रश्न बनाते समय सहायक क्रिया 'Do' या 'Does' को कर्ता (Subject) से पहले लाना अनिवार्य होता है। अगर प्रश्न 'हाँ/ना' वाला हो, तो शुरुआत 'Do' या 'Does' से होगी (जैसे: 'Do you drink tea?'). अगर कोई जानकारी पूछनी हो, तो पहले WH- शब्द आएगा, फिर Do/Does, फिर Subject और फिर मुख्य क्रिया का मूल रूप (जैसे: 'Where do you live?').

**English Explanation**:
In English Present Simple, questions with lexical verbs require subject-auxiliary inversion using the DO-support system. For Yes/No questions, 'Do' or 'Does' precedes the subject, and the main verb stays in its bare infinitive form. For informational questions, the question word (WH- element) comes first, followed immediately by DO/DOES, the subject, and the base verb. A vital rule for spoken English: never answer a Do/Does question with the BE verb ('*Yes, I am*') or a bare main verb ('*Yes, I live*'); always use the auxiliary operator ('Yes, I do').

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Yes/No Question Formation (Do vs. Does)** [Core Structural Rule]
  Formula: [Do / Does] + [Subject] + [Base Verb (V1)] + [Object/Complement]? | Use 'Do' with I/you/we/they; use 'Does' with he/she/it.
- **Rule 2: Natural Short Answers Formula** [Conversational Pragmatics]
  Affirmative: 'Yes, + pronoun + do/does.' Negative: 'No, + pronoun + don't/doesn't.' Do not repeat the main verb or use BE.
- **Rule 3: WH- Question Inversion Order (QUASM)** [Universal Grammar Standard]
  Formula: [Question Word] + [Auxiliary (Do/Does)] + [Subject] + [Main Verb (V1)]? | Never omit the auxiliary operator.
- **Rule 4: Base Verb Rule in Interrogatives** [Syntax Precision]
  Just like in negatives, 'Does' already handles the 3rd person singular '-s'. The main verb MUST be in base form. Correct: 'Does he know?' Incorrect: '*Does he knows?*'

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "type": "Yes/No Question (Plural/I/You)",
    "formula": "Do + [I / you / we / they] + Base Verb + ...?",
    "exampleEn": "Do you live nearby?",
    "meaningHi": "क्या आप पास में रहते हैं?"
  },
  {
    "type": "Yes/No Question (Singular)",
    "formula": "Does + [he / she / it / Name] + Base Verb + ...?",
    "exampleEn": "Does Priya drive to work?",
    "meaningHi": "क्या प्रिया काम पर गाड़ी से जाती है?"
  },
  {
    "type": "Short Answer (Affirmative)",
    "formula": "Yes, + [Subject Pronoun] + do / does.",
    "exampleEn": "Yes, I do. / Yes, she does.",
    "meaningHi": "हाँ, मैं रहता हूँ। / हाँ, वह जाती है।"
  },
  {
    "type": "Short Answer (Negative)",
    "formula": "No, + [Subject Pronoun] + don't / doesn't.",
    "exampleEn": "No, we don't. / No, he doesn't.",
    "meaningHi": "नहीं, हम नहीं रहते। / नहीं, वह नहीं जाता।"
  },
  {
    "type": "WH- Question Structure",
    "formula": "WH- Word + do / does + Subject + Base Verb + ...?",
    "exampleEn": "Where do you work?",
    "meaningHi": "आप कहाँ काम करते हैं?"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Omitting the Auxiliary ('Where you live?') and Mismatched Short Answers ('Yes, I am')
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'आप कहाँ रहते हैं?' में कोई सहायक क्रिया नहीं लगती, इसलिए शिक्षार्थी अंग्रेज़ी में 'Where you live?' बोल देते हैं। इसके अलावा 'Do you work here?' के जवाब में 'Yes, I am' बोल देते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*Where you work?*' instead of 'Where do you work?', and answering 'Do you like tea?' with '*Yes, I am*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: Without 'do/does', the sentence is an ungrammatical statement fragment in English. Furthermore, the short answer must match the auxiliary of the question: 'Do you...?' -> 'Yes, I do.'
- **Natural English Solution**: **Always insert 'do/does' after the WH- word: 'Where DO you work?' and reply: 'Yes, I do.'**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day12_vocab_001` | **Live** | Verb | `/lɪv/` | रहना / निवास करना | Where do you live? <br> *आप कहाँ रहते हैं?* |
| `day12_vocab_002` | **Travel** | Verb | `/ˈtræv.əl/` | यात्रा करना | How often do you travel abroad? <br> *आप कितनी बार विदेश यात्रा करते हैं?* |
| `day12_vocab_003` | **Spend** | Verb | `/spend/` | खर्च करना / बिताना (समय) | How much time do you spend on your studies? <br> *आप अपनी पढ़ाई पर कितना समय बिताते हैं?* |
| `day12_vocab_004` | **Leave** | Verb | `/liːv/` | निकलना / प्रस्थान करना | What time does the train leave? <br> *ट्रेन किस समय निकलती है?* |
| `day12_vocab_005` | **Arrive** | Verb | `/əˈraɪv/` | पहुँचना / आगमन होना | When does the flight arrive? <br> *उड़ान कब पहुँचती है?* |
| `day12_vocab_006` | **Mean** | Verb | `/miːn/` | अर्थ होना / मतलब होना | What does this word mean? <br> *इस शब्द का क्या मतलब है?* |
| `day12_vocab_007` | **Happen** | Verb | `/ˈhæp.ən/` | घटित होना / होना | Why does this error happen often? <br> *यह त्रुटि बार-बार क्यों होती है?* |
| `day12_vocab_008` | **Study** | Verb | `/ˈstʌd.i/` | अध्ययन करना / पढ़ना | Do you study in the evening? <br> *क्या आप शाम को पढ़ाई करते हैं?* |
| `day12_vocab_009` | **Cost** | Verb | `/kɒst/` | कीमत होना | How much does a monthly metro pass cost? <br> *मासिक मेट्रो पास की कीमत कितनी होती है?* |
| `day12_vocab_010` | **Prefer** | Verb | `/prɪˈfɜːr/` | अधिक पसंद करना / प्राथमिकता देना | Do you prefer tea or coffee? <br> *क्या आप चाय पसंद करते हैं या कॉफ़ी?* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day12_phrase_001` [Polite / Social Inquiry]: **"What do you do for a living?"** — *आप आजीविका के लिए क्या काम करते हैं?*
- `day12_phrase_002` [Neutral]: **"What does that mean?"** — *उसका क्या मतलब है?*
- `day12_phrase_003` [Neutral]: **"What time does it start?"** — *यह कितने बजे शुरू होता है?*
- `day12_phrase_004` [Formal / Administrative]: **"How do you spell your surname?"** — *आप अपने उपनाम की वर्तनी (spelling) कैसे लिखते हैं?*
- `day12_phrase_005` [Conversational / Clarification]: **"Does that make sense to you?"** — *क्या यह बात आपकी समझ में आ रही है?*
- `day12_phrase_006` [Neutral]: **"How often do you go there?"** — *आप वहाँ कितनी बार जाते हैं?*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day12_example_001`: **Do you speak English at work? — Yes, I do.**  
  *क्या आप काम पर अंग्रेज़ी बोलते हैं? — हाँ, मैं बोलता हूँ।*
- `day12_example_002`: **Does your sister work in healthcare? — No, she doesn't.**  
  *क्या आपकी बहन स्वास्थ्य क्षेत्र में काम करती है? — नहीं, वह नहीं करती।*
- `day12_example_003`: **Where do they usually buy their groceries?**  
  *वे आमतौर पर अपना राशन कहाँ से खरीदते हैं?*
- `day12_example_004`: **What time does the supermarket open in the morning?**  
  *सुपरमार्केट सुबह कितने बजे खुलता है?*
- `day12_example_005`: **Why does he wake up so early every single day?**  
  *वह हर रोज़ इतनी सुबह क्यों उठता है?*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Rising intonation (↗) at the end of Yes/No questions: 'Do you live here? ↗', Falling intonation (↘) at the end of WH- questions: 'Where do you work? ↘', Assimilation of 'Do you' into smooth /dʒuː/ or /djʊ/
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do NOT use a falling tone on Yes/No questions; it sounds like a blunt command. Raise your pitch slightly at the final word.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day12_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "Do you drink coffee in the morning? ↗"
- `day12_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "Does he understand the rules? ↗"
- `day12_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "Where do you want to go? ↘"
- `day12_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "What time does the bus leave? ↘"

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day12_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `YES_NO_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct question to ask 'क्या आप यहाँ काम करते हैं?':
**Options**: `Are you work here?` | `Do you work here?` | `Do you works here?` | `You work here?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Do you work here?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'Do' + Subject (you) + Base Verb (work).
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'You' के साथ प्रश्न में 'Do you work here?' आता है।

#### 2. `[day12_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `YES_NO_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct question for 'क्या राहुल गाड़ी चलाता है?':
**Options**: `Does Rahul drives?` | `Do Rahul drive?` | `Does Rahul drive?` | `Is Rahul drive?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Does Rahul drive?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Rahul' is singular, so use 'Does' and the base verb 'drive' without '-s'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: एकवचन नाम के लिए 'Does' लगेगा और 'drive' में 's' नहीं लगेगा।

#### 3. `[day12_ex_003]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `SHORT_ANSWERS`)
**Prompt**: What is the correct short answer to: 'Do you live in Mumbai?'
**Options**: `Yes, I am.` | `Yes, I live.` | `Yes, I do.` | `Yes, I do live.`
- **Correct Answer**: `Yes, I do.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Present Simple 'Do you...?' questions are answered with 'Yes, I do' or 'No, I don't'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Do you...?' का सही उत्तर 'Yes, I do.' होता है, 'Yes, I am' कभी नहीं।

#### 4. `[day12_ex_004]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `WH_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Select the correct question for 'आप कहाँ रहते हैं?':
**Options**: `Where you live?` | `Where do you live?` | `Where does you live?` | `Where are you live?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Where do you live?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: The formula is WH-word (Where) + Auxiliary (do) + Subject (you) + Verb (live).
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: Where do you live?

#### 5. `[day12_ex_005]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `WH_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct question: 'यह दुकान कब बंद होती है?'
**Options**: `When do this shop close?` | `When does this shop close?` | `When this shop closes?` | `When is this shop close?`
- **Correct Answer**: `When does this shop close?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'This shop' is singular (it), requiring the auxiliary 'does'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'This shop' एकवचन है, इसलिए 'When does this shop close?' सही है।

#### 6. `[day12_ex_006]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: ______ (Do / Does) your parents live with you?
- **Correct Answer**: `Do`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Your parents' is plural, so use 'Do'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Your parents' बहुवचन है, इसलिए 'Do' आएगा।

#### 7. `[day12_ex_007]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: What time ______ (do / does) the meeting start?
- **Correct Answer**: `does`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'The meeting' is singular (it), so use 'does'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'The meeting' एकवचन है, इसलिए 'does' आएगा।

#### 8. `[day12_ex_008]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: Why ______ (do / does) they leave the office so early?
- **Correct Answer**: `do`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'They' takes the auxiliary 'do'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'They' के साथ 'do' का प्रयोग होता है।

#### 9. `[day12_ex_009]` [FILL_BLANK] (medium | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: ______ (Does / Is) he have a two-wheeler?
- **Correct Answer**: `Does`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'Does he have' for asking about possession in Present Simple.
- **Explanation (HI)**: अधिकार या स्वामित्व पूछने के लिए 'Does he have...?' आता है।

#### 10. `[day12_ex_010]` [FILL_BLANK] (medium | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: How much ______ (do / does) a ticket to Jaipur cost?
- **Correct Answer**: `does`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'A ticket' is singular, so use 'does'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'A ticket' एकवचन है, इसलिए 'does' आएगा।

#### 11. `[day12_ex_011]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the syntax error: 'Where you work in Bangalore?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Where do you work in Bangalore?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Insert the auxiliary 'do' between 'Where' and 'you'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Where' और 'you' के बीच 'do' लगाना आवश्यक है: 'Where do you work in Bangalore?'

#### 12. `[day12_ex_012]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'Does Amit speaks German?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Does Amit speak German?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: The main verb must be in base form 'speak' after 'Does'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Does' आने पर 'speaks' का 's' हट जाता है: 'Does Amit speak German?'

#### 13. `[day12_ex_013]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the short answer: 'Do you study every day? — Yes, I am.'
- **Correct Answer**: `Do you study every day? — Yes, I do.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Match the auxiliary: question with 'Do you...?' must be answered with 'Yes, I do.'
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Do you...?' प्रश्न का उत्तर 'Yes, I do.' होता है।

#### 14. `[day12_ex_014]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'What time does the train leaves?'
- **Correct Answer**: `What time does the train leave?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use base verb 'leave' after 'does', not 'leaves'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Does' के बाद 'leave' आएगा, 'leaves' नहीं।

#### 15. `[day12_ex_015]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'How often does you visit your hometown?'
- **Correct Answer**: `How often do you visit your hometown?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'You' requires 'do', not 'does'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'You' के साथ 'do' आता है, 'does' नहीं।

#### 16. `[day12_ex_016]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (medium | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words to form a correct WH- question:
- **Correct Answer**: `What do you do for a living`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Question structure: What (WH) + do (Aux) + you (Subject) + do (Main Verb) + for a living?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: What do you do for a living?

#### 17. `[day12_ex_017]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a correct question:
- **Correct Answer**: `Does she like classical music`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Does + Subject (she) + Verb (like) + Object (classical music)?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: Does she like classical music?

#### 18. `[day12_ex_018]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a correct question:
- **Correct Answer**: `What time does the class start`
- **Explanation (EN)**: What time (WH) + does (Aux) + the class (Subject) + start (Verb)?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: What time does the class start?

#### 19. `[day12_ex_019]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a correct question:
- **Correct Answer**: `Where do you buy your groceries`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Where + do + you + buy + your groceries?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: Where do you buy your groceries?

#### 20. `[day12_ex_020]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a correct question:
- **Correct Answer**: `When does the flight arrive`
- **Explanation (EN)**: When + does + the flight + arrive?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: When does the flight arrive?

#### 21. `[day12_ex_021]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'क्या आप सप्ताहांत में काम करते हैं?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Do you work on weekends?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Do you work on the weekend?`, `Do you work at weekends?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Do you work on weekends?' is natural English.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Do you work on weekends?' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 22. `[day12_ex_022]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (medium | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'वह रोज़ कितने बजे उठता है?'
- **Correct Answer**: `What time does he wake up every day?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `What time does he get up every day?`, `When does he wake up every day?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'What time does he wake up every day?' uses WH- question order with 'does'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'What time does he wake up every day?' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 23. `[day12_ex_023]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'इस शब्द का क्या मतलब है?'
- **Correct Answer**: `What does this word mean?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `What does this word mean`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'What does this word mean?' is the standard English inquiry.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'What does this word mean?' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 24. `[day12_ex_024]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'क्या वे अंग्रेज़ी समझते हैं?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Do they understand English?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Do they understand English`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject 'they' takes 'Do they understand English?'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Do they understand English?' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 25. `[day12_ex_025]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (medium | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'आप अपनी छुट्टियाँ कहाँ बिताते हैं?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Where do you spend your holidays?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Where do you spend your vacations?`, `Where do you spend your holiday?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Where do you spend your holidays?' follows QUASM order.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Where do you spend your holidays?' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 26. `[day12_ex_026]` [TRANSLATION_EN_HI] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into natural Hindi: 'How often do you exercise?'
- **Correct Answer**: `आप कितनी बार व्यायाम करते हैं?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `आप कितनी बार कसरत करते हैं?`, `तुम कितनी बार व्यायाम करते हो?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'How often' translates to 'कितनी बार'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'How often do you exercise?' का अर्थ है 'आप कितनी बार व्यायाम/कसरत करते हैं?'

#### 27. `[day12_ex_027]` [TRANSLATION_EN_HI] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into natural Hindi: 'Does your brother live in Canada?'
- **Correct Answer**: `क्या आपका भाई कनाडा में रहता है?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `क्या तुम्हारा भाई कनाडा में रहता है?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Does your brother live...' is a Yes/No question in Present Simple.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'क्या आपका भाई कनाडा में रहता है?' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 28. `[day12_ex_028]` [LISTENING_DICTATION] (medium | `LISTENING`)
**Prompt**: Listen to the audio and write down the question exactly as spoken:
- **Correct Answer**: `What time do you usually finish work?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `What time do you usually finish work`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Listen for the WH- phrase 'What time', auxiliary 'do', subject 'you', and adverb 'usually'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'What time do you usually finish work?' ध्यान से सुनकर लिखें।

#### 29. `[day12_ex_029]` [LISTENING_DICTATION] (medium | `LISTENING`)
**Prompt**: Listen to the audio and write down the short conversation:
- **Correct Answer**: `Does she speak Hindi? — Yes, she does.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Does she speak Hindi? Yes, she does.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Note the question with 'Does she' and the matching short answer 'Yes, she does.'
- **Explanation (HI)**: प्रश्न और उत्तर दोनों को सही विराम चिह्नों के साथ लिखें।

#### 30. `[day12_ex_030]` [MATCHING] (easy | `MATCHING`)
**Prompt**: Match the WH- question words with their correct meaning and inquiry type:
- **Correct Answer**: `Matched correctly`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Each WH- word targets a specific category of information.
- **Explanation (HI)**: प्रत्येक WH- शब्द का उसकी सही जानकारी के प्रकार से मिलान करें।

#### 31. `[day12_ex_031]` [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (easy | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the question: Receptionist: 'Welcome to our clinic. ______ (you / have) an appointment today?' Patient: 'Yes, I do. At 4:30 PM.'
- **Correct Answer**: `Do you have`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'Do you have' to ask if someone possesses an appointment.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Do you have' सही प्रश्न रूप है।

#### 32. `[day12_ex_032]` [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (medium | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the question: Tourist: 'Excuse me, ______ (how / I / get) to the city center from here?' Local: 'You can take the number 5 bus.'
- **Correct Answer**: `how do I get`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `how do I reach`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'How do I get to...' is the standard phrase for asking travel directions.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'How do I get' रास्ता पूछने का मानक वाक्यांश है।

#### 33. `[day12_ex_033]` [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (easy | `SPEAKING`)
**Prompt**: Speak aloud: Ask a polite question to find out where your colleague lives, using 'Where do you...?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Where do you live?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'Where do you live?' with a polite falling intonation.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Where do you live?' बोलकर अभ्यास करें।

#### 34. `[day12_ex_034]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Convert the statement into a question: 'Neha drinks green tea.'
- **Correct Answer**: `Does Neha drink green tea?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Does Neha drink green tea`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Invert to 'Does Neha drink green tea?'. Remember to drop the '-s' on 'drink'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Does Neha drink green tea?' सही प्रश्न रूप है।

#### 35. `[day12_ex_035]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Convert the statement into a question: 'They play cricket on Sundays.'
- **Correct Answer**: `Do they play cricket on Sundays?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Do they play cricket on Sundays`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'Do they play cricket on Sundays?'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Do they play cricket on Sundays?' सही प्रश्न रूप है।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Networking at a technology conference: exchanging background, role details, and daily work habits.

#### Dialogue (`day12_dialogue_001`):
- **Ananya**: "Hello! My name is Ananya. Do you mind if I join you?"  
  *(नमस्ते! मेरा नाम अनन्या है। क्या मैं यहाँ बैठ सकती हूँ?)*
- **Marcus**: "Not at all, Ananya! Please have a seat. I'm Marcus."  
  *(बिल्कुल बैठिए, अनन्या! कृपया बैठें। मैं मार्कस हूँ।)*
- **Ananya**: "Nice to meet you, Marcus. What do you do?"  
  *(आपसे मिलकर खुशी हुई, मार्कस। आप क्या काम करते हैं?)*
- **Marcus**: "I work as a UI designer at a tech startup. Where do you work?"  
  *(मैं एक टेक स्टार्टअप में यूआई डिज़ाइनर के रूप में काम करता हूँ। आप कहाँ काम करती हैं?)*
- **Ananya**: "I work for an education company in Delhi. Do you travel to India often?"  
  *(मैं दिल्ली में एक शिक्षा कंपनी के लिए काम करती हूँ। क्या आप अक्सर भारत यात्रा करते हैं?)*
- **Marcus**: "No, I don't, but I really want to visit soon!"  
  *(नहीं, मैं अक्सर नहीं जाता, लेकिन मैं जल्द ही वहाँ जाना चाहता हूँ!)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Record yourself asking 3 questions to a new colleague: 1) What they do for a living, 2) Where they live, and 3) Whether they speak English or Hindi at home.
- **HI**: एक नए सहकर्मी से 3 प्रश्न पूछने का ऑडियो रिकॉर्ड करें: 1) वे क्या काम करते हैं, 2) वे कहाँ रहते हैं, 3) क्या वे घर पर अंग्रेज़ी या हिन्दी बोलते हैं।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day12_assess_001]` **Why is 'Where you live?' incorrect in standard English?** ➔ *Because Present Simple WH- questions require auxiliary inversion with 'do/does' (Where DO you live?).* [Skill: Syntax Precision]
- `[day12_assess_002]` **What is wrong with answering 'Do you drive?' with 'Yes, I am'?** ➔ *'Am' is a BE verb; questions formed with the auxiliary 'Do' must be answered using the auxiliary 'do' ('Yes, I do').* [Skill: Pragmatic Competence]
- `[day12_assess_003]` **What happens to the '-s' on the main verb when forming a question with 'Does'?** ➔ *The '-s' is dropped because 'Does' already marks the third-person singular (e.g., 'Does he know?', not '*Does he knows?*').* [Skill: Morphological Precision]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `PRESENT_SIMPLE_QUESTIONS_L1`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 13: Food, Drinks & Ordering at a Café (Some / Any / A / An & Quantifiers) {#day-13-day_13}
**शीर्षक**: खान-पान, मात्रा और कैफ़े में ऑर्डर करना (Some / Any / A / An तथा गणनीय/अगणनीय संज्ञाएं)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.1` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 02 (Polite Phrases), Day 08 (Demonstratives), Day 11–12 (Present Simple)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Differentiate countable nouns (an apple, two sandwiches) from uncountable mass nouns (water, tea, bread, sugar).
  - **HI**: गिनने योग्य (Countable) और न गिनने योग्य (Uncountable) वस्तुओं का अंतर समझना।
- **EN**: Apply 'some' in affirmative sentences and polite requests/offers ('Can I have some water?').
  - **HI**: सकारात्मक वाक्यों और विनम्र अनुरोध/प्रस्ताव में 'some' का प्रयोग करना (e.g. Can I have some tea?)।
- **EN**: Apply 'any' in negative statements and open questions ('We don't have any milk', 'Do you have any sugar?').
  - **HI**: नकारात्मक वाक्यों और सामान्य प्रश्नों में 'any' का प्रयोग करना (e.g. We don't have any juice)।
- **EN**: Order food and beverages politely in a café using formulaic chunks ('I'd like...', 'Can I have...?').
  - **HI**: कैफ़े या रेस्तरां में विनम्रता से ऑर्डर देना (जैसे: 'I'd like a cup of coffee, please')।
- **EN**: Eliminate direct Hindi transfer errors such as '*give one water*' or '*I want a bread*'.
  - **HI**: हिन्दी से सीधे अनुवाद की गलतियाँ जैसे '*एक पानी देना*' (*a water*) या '*a bread*' से बचना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
अंग्रेज़ी में संज्ञाएं (Nouns) दो प्रकार की होती हैं: 1) Countable (जिन्हें एक, दो, तीन गिन सकते हैं, जैसे: apple, cup, sandwich) और 2) Uncountable (जिन्हें अलग-अलग नहीं गिन सकते, जैसे: water, milk, bread, sugar, tea)। Countable एकवचन के साथ 'a' या 'an' लगता है। Uncountable के साथ 'a' या 'an' कभी नहीं लगता; उनके लिए हम 'some' या माप की इकाई (a cup of tea, a bottle of water, a slice of bread) का प्रयोग करते हैं। सकारात्मक वाक्यों में 'some' आता है ('I have some tea'), लेकिन नकारात्मक और सामान्य प्रश्नों में 'any' आता है ('I don't have any tea', 'Do you have any tea? )। अपवाद: जब हम विनम्रता से कुछ मांगते हैं या किसी को कुछ पेश करते हैं, तो प्रश्न में भी 'some' आता है ('Can I have some water, please? )।

**English Explanation**:
In English, nouns are strictly classified into countable (discrete items taking singular a/an or plural -s) and uncountable (continuous mass/liquid/abstract nouns with no plural form). Uncountable nouns cannot take 'a/an' directly (saying '*a bread*' or '*a milk*' is ungrammatical). We quantify them using 'some' or partitive container phrases ('a bottle of water', 'a piece of bread'). For determiners: use 'some' in positive statements, 'any' in negative sentences and general questions. Crucially, when making a polite request or offer, use 'some' in questions ('Would you like some tea?' / 'Can I have some sugar?').

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Countable vs. Uncountable Distinction** [Core Lexical Rule]
  Countable: Singular takes 'a/an' (a banana, a cup). Plural takes '-s' (two bananas). Uncountable: Takes NO 'a/an' and has NO plural (water, coffee, bread, cheese).
- **Rule 2: The Some vs. Any Paradigm** [Universal Grammar Standard]
  Affirmative: 'We have some apples / some milk.' Negative: 'We don't have any apples / any milk.' General Question: 'Do you have any apples / any milk?'
- **Rule 3: The Polite Request Exception for 'Some'** [Pragmatic Politeness]
  In questions that represent a polite request or offer where the expected answer is 'yes', use 'some' instead of 'any'. (e.g., 'Can I have some water, please?' / 'Would you like some cookies?')
- **Rule 4: Partitives and Container Units** [Spoken & Written Accuracy]
  To count uncountable liquids and foods, use partitives: 'a cup of [tea]', 'a bottle of [water]', 'a slice of [bread/cake]', 'a bowl of [soup]'.

```json
  },
  {

  },
  {
    "category": "Negative (Any Noun)",

  },
  {

  },
  {

  }

```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Treating 'Bread', 'Water', and 'Information' as Countables ('Give one bread / one water')
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में हम 'एक ब्रेड' या 'एक पानी देना' आसानी से बोल देते हैं, इसलिए भारतीय शिक्षार्थी अंग्रेज़ी में 'Give a bread' या 'Give a water' बोल देते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*I want a bread*' or '*Do you have a water?*'`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: In English, 'bread' and 'water' are mass nouns. You cannot put 'a' before them. You must say 'a slice of bread' or 'a bottle/glass of water', or simply 'some bread / some water'.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Say 'Can I have a slice of bread?' or 'Can I get some water, please?'**

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day13_phrase_001` [Polite Café Formula]: **"I'd like a cup of coffee, please."** — *मुझे एक कप कॉफ़ी चाहिए, कृपया।*
- `day13_phrase_002` [Polite Restaurant Formula]: **"Can I have the bill, please?"** — *क्या मुझे बिल मिल सकता है, कृपया?*
- `day13_phrase_003` [Café Cashier Formula]: **"For here or to go / takeaway?"** — *यहीं खाएंगे या पैक करवाएंगे?*
- `day13_phrase_004` [Service Formula]: **"Would you like anything else?"** — *क्या आप कुछ और लेना चाहेंगे?*
- `day13_phrase_005` [Inquiry Formula]: **"Do you have any vegetarian options?"** — *क्या आपके पास कोई शाकाहारी विकल्प हैं?*
- `day13_phrase_006` [Tipping / Politeness]: **"Keep the change, thank you."** — *बाकी खुले पैसे आप रख लीजिए, धन्यवाद।*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day13_example_001`: **I would like a cappuccino and two croissants, please.**  
  *मुझे एक कैपुचीनो और दो क्रोइसैन चाहिए, कृपया।*
- `day13_example_002`: **Do you have any almond milk available today?**  
  *क्या आज आपके पास बादाम का दूध उपलब्ध है?*
- `day13_example_003`: **We don't have any sugar left in the kitchen.**  
  *रसोई में बिल्कुल भी चीनी नहीं बची है।*
- `day13_example_004`: **Can you bring me a bottle of mineral water, please?**  
  *क्या आप मेरे लिए एक बोतल मिनरल वाटर ला सकते हैं, कृपया?*
- `day13_example_005`: **Would you like some extra butter on your toast?**  
  *क्या आप अपने टोस्ट पर थोड़ा अतिरिक्त मक्खन लेना चाहेंगे?*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Smooth contraction /aɪd laɪk/ for 'I'd like', Linking consonant to vowel: 'cup of' -> /ˈkʌp.əv/, 'glass of' -> /ˈɡlɑːs.əv/, Soft /dʒ/ in 'juice' /dʒuːs/ and 'sandwich' /ˈsæn.wɪdʒ/
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do NOT pronounce 'sandwich' as 'sand-witch' with a heavy /tʃ/. Let the end be a soft /dʒ/ or /tʃ/ without vowel elongation.

- `day13_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "I'd like a cup of tea, please."
- `day13_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "Can I have some water?"
- `day13_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "We don't have any milk left."
- `day13_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "Could we have the check, please?"

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day13_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `POLITE_ORDERING`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct phrase to order at a café counter:
**Options**: `I want a coffee.` | `I'd like a cup of coffee, please.` | `Give me one coffee.` | `I like a coffee now.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I'd like a cup of coffee, please.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'I'd like a cup of coffee, please' is the most polite, standard international phrasing.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'I'd like a cup of coffee, please' सबसे विनम्र और मानक रूप है।

#### 2. `[day13_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `SOME_ANY_RULES`)
**Prompt**: Select the correct sentence with 'some / any':
**Options**: `We don't have some milk.` | `We don't have any milk.` | `We don't have a milk.` | `We doesn't have any milk.`
- **Correct Answer**: `We don't have any milk.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'any' with uncountable nouns in negative sentences.
- **Explanation (HI)**: नकारात्मक वाक्य में अगणनीय संज्ञा (milk) के साथ 'any' आता है।

#### 3. `[day13_ex_003]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `POLITE_REQUESTS`)
**Prompt**: Which question is a polite request asking for water?
**Options**: `Can I have some water, please?` | `Can I have any water, please?` | `Do I have some water?` | `Give me some water!`
- **Correct Answer**: `Can I have some water, please?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: In polite requests, 'some' is used instead of 'any'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: विनम्र अनुरोध (Request) में 'any' की जगह 'some' का प्रयोग होता है।

#### 4. `[day13_ex_004]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `COUNTABLE_UNCOUNTABLE`)
**Prompt**: Identify the UNCOUNTABLE noun that cannot take 'a' directly:
**Options**: `Apple` | `Sandwich` | `Bread` | `Banana`
- **Correct Answer**: `Bread`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Bread' is an uncountable mass noun in English; you say 'a slice of bread' or 'some bread'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Bread' अंग्रेज़ी में Uncountable है; इसे 'a bread' नहीं कहा जा सकता।

#### 5. `[day13_ex_005]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the cashier's question: 'Is that for here or ______?'
**Options**: `to going` | `to go` | `to gone` | `for going`
- **Correct Answer**: `to go`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'For here or to go?' (or 'takeaway') is the standard customer service phrase.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'For here or to go?' कैफ़े में पूछा जाने वाला मानक वाक्यांश है।

#### 6. `[day13_ex_006]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: Do you have ______ (some / any) vegetarian sandwiches?
- **Correct Answer**: `any`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'any' in standard informational questions.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सामान्य प्रश्न में 'any' का प्रयोग करें।

#### 7. `[day13_ex_007]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: There is ______ (a / some) butter in the refrigerator.
- **Correct Answer**: `some`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Butter' is uncountable and affirmative, so use 'some'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Butter' अगणनीय है, इसलिए 'some' आएगा।

- **Explanation (EN)**: 'For here or takeaway' asks whether the food is consumed on-site or packed.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'क्या आप यहीं खाएंगे या पैक करवाएंगे?' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 27. `[day13_ex_027]` [TRANSLATION_EN_HI] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into natural Hindi: 'We have some fresh sandwiches.'
- **Correct Answer**: `हमारे पास कुछ ताज़े सैंडविच हैं।`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `हमारे पास ताज़े सैंडविच हैं।`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'We have some fresh sandwiches' translates to 'हमारे पास कुछ ताज़े सैंडविच हैं।'
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'हमारे पास कुछ ताज़े सैंडविच हैं।' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 28. `[day13_ex_028]` [LISTENING_DICTATION] (medium | `LISTENING`)
**Prompt**: Listen to the audio and write down the order:
- **Correct Answer**: `I'd like a cappuccino and a bottle of mineral water, please.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `I would like a cappuccino and a bottle of mineral water, please.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Listen for the contraction 'I'd like' and the partitive 'a bottle of mineral water'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: ऑडियो को ध्यान से सुनकर पूरा वाक्य लिखें।

#### 29. `[day13_ex_029]` [LISTENING_DICTATION] (medium | `LISTENING`)
**Prompt**: Listen to the cashier and write the question:
- **Correct Answer**: `Would you like anything else with your coffee?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Would you like anything else with your coffee`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Note the polite service question: 'Would you like anything else with...'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Would you like anything else with your coffee?' सुनकर लिखें।

#### 30. `[day13_ex_030]` [MATCHING] (easy | `MATCHING`)
**Prompt**: Match the food nouns with their correct quantifier category:
- **Correct Answer**: `Matched correctly`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Identify countable nouns vs. mass uncountable nouns.
- **Explanation (HI)**: प्रत्येक खाद्य पदार्थ का उसके सही गणनीय/अगणनीय वर्ग से मिलान करें।

#### 31. `[day13_ex_031]` [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (easy | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the café interaction: Barista: 'Good morning! What can I get for you today?' Customer: '______ (I / like) a large black Americano, please.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I'd like`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `I would like`, `Can I have`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'I'd like' or 'I would like' to place your order politely.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'I'd like' ऑर्डर देने का सबसे स्वाभाविक तरीका है।

#### 32. `[day13_ex_032]` [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (easy | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the interaction: Customer: 'Do you have any skim milk?' Barista: 'I'm sorry, we ______ (not / have) any skim milk left today.'
- **Correct Answer**: `don't have`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `do not have`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'don't have' with the subject 'we'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'We' के साथ 'don't have' आएगा।

#### 33. `[day13_ex_033]` [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (easy | `SPEAKING`)
**Prompt**: Speak aloud: Role-play ordering a hot drink and a snack at a coffee shop using 'I'd like a... and a..., please.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I'd like a [drink] and a [snack], please.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: State your drink and food item clearly with polite intonation.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'I'd like a...' का प्रयोग करके अपना ऑर्डर बोलें।

#### 34. `[day13_ex_034]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Why can you NOT say 'Give me a water' in standard English?
- **Correct Answer**: `Because water is an uncountable mass noun; you must say 'some water' or 'a glass/bottle of water'.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Uncountable nouns cannot take the indefinite article 'a/an'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: पानी (water) अगणनीय है, इसलिए इसके आगे सीधे 'a' नहीं लगता।

#### 35. `[day13_ex_035]` [RECALL] (medium | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Fill in the blank with 'some' or 'any': 'Would you like ______ cookies with your tea?'
- **Correct Answer**: `some`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Some' is used in polite offers expecting an affirmative response.
- **Explanation (HI)**: विनम्र प्रस्तावों में 'some' आता है।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Ordering breakfast and drinks at a busy airport café during a morning layover.

#### Dialogue (`day13_dialogue_001`):
- **Barista**: "Good morning! Welcome to Transit Coffee. What can I get started for you?"  
  *(शुभ प्रभात! ट्रांज़िट कॉफ़ी में स्वागत है। मैं आपके लिए क्या ला सकता हूँ?)*
- **Vikram**: "Good morning! I'd like a large cappuccino with oat milk, please."  
  *(शुभ प्रभात! मुझे ओट मिल्क (जई के दूध) के साथ एक बड़ा कैपुचीनो चाहिए, कृपया।)*
- **Barista**: "Sure thing. Would you like any pastries or warm sandwiches today?"  
  *(ज़रूर। क्या आप आज कोई पेस्ट्री या गर्म सैंडविच लेना चाहेंगे?)*
- **Vikram**: "Yes, can I have a toasted cheese sandwich and a bottle of still water?"  
  *(हाँ, क्या मुझे एक टोस्टेड चीज़ सैंडविच और एक बोतल सादा पानी मिल सकता है?)*
- **Barista**: "Certainly. For here or to go?"  
  *(बिल्कुल। यहीं लेंगे या ले जाने के लिए?)*
- **Vikram**: "To go, please. How much is that altogether?"  
  *(ले जाने के लिए, कृपया। कुल मिलाकर कितने पैसे हुए?)*
- **Barista**: "That's eight pounds fifty. You can tap your card right here."  
  *(साढ़े आठ पाउंड हुए। आप यहाँ अपना कार्ड टैप कर सकते हैं।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Record a full café ordering dialogue: greet the barista, order one beverage with a specific preparation (e.g. coffee with milk, no sugar), request one food item, and ask for the total price.
- **HI**: एक पूरा कैफ़े ऑर्डर संवाद रिकॉर्ड करें: अभिवादन करें, एक पेय पदार्थ का ऑर्डर दें, एक खाने की चीज़ मांगें और कुल कीमत पूछें।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day13_assess_001]` **Why is 'We don't have some milk' incorrect in standard English?** ➔ *Because negative sentences require the non-assertive determiner 'any' (We don't have ANY milk).* [Skill: Syntax Precision]
- `[day13_assess_002]` **When is 'some' used in a question instead of 'any'?** ➔ *In polite requests ('Can I have some water?') and polite offers ('Would you like some tea?').* [Skill: Pragmatic Competence]
- `[day13_assess_003]` **How do you count uncountable nouns like bread, water, or paper?** ➔ *By using partitives and container units: a slice of bread, a bottle of water, a sheet of paper.* [Skill: Partitive Mechanics]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `COUNTABLE_UNCOUNTABLE_SOME_ANY_L1`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 14: Hobbies, Preferences & Free Time (Like / Love / Hate + -ing & Stative Preferences) {#day-14-day_14}
**शीर्षक**: शौक, पसंद-नापसंद और खाली समय (Like / Love / Hate + -ing तथा Prefer X to Y)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.1` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 08–09 (Present Simple), Day 11–12 (Negatives & Questions)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Express personal hobbies, likes, and dislikes using preference verbs followed by gerunds (like / love / enjoy / hate + Verb-ING).
  - **HI**: अपनी पसंद, नापसंद और शौक को 'like / love / enjoy / hate + क्रिया-ING' के साथ व्यक्त करना सीखना।
- **EN**: Master the comparative preference formula 'prefer [X] to [Y]' (e.g., 'I prefer coffee to tea', NEVER 'prefer than').
  - **HI**: 'Prefer X to Y' का सही नियम सीखना (जैसे: 'I prefer tea to coffee', 'than' का प्रयोग कभी नहीं करना)।
- **EN**: Describe free-time activities fluently with frequency and time adverbs ('in my spare time', 'on weekends').
  - **HI**: खाली समय की गतिविधियों का वर्णन करना ('in my free time', 'on Sundays')।
- **EN**: Eliminate the common Indian English transfer error of omitting the gerund (saying '*I like cook*' instead of 'I like cooking').
  - **HI**: हिन्दी के प्रभाव से क्रिया में से '-ing' छोड़ देने की गलती (जैसे: '*I like cook*') को पूरी तरह समाप्त करना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
जब हम अपनी पसंद या शौक बताते हैं (जैसे: 'मुझे किताबें पढ़ना पसंद है', 'उसे गाने सुनना अच्छा लगता है'), तो अंग्रेज़ी में पसंद बताने वाली क्रियाओं (like, love, enjoy, hate, dislike) के बाद आने वाली मुख्य क्रिया में अनिवार्य रूप से '-ing' (Gerund) लगाया जाता है (जैसे: 'I like reading books', 'She loves listening to music')। केवल 'I like read' कहना व्याकरण की दृष्टि से गलत है। दूसरा सबसे महत्वपूर्ण नियम: जब हम एक चीज़ के मुकाबले दूसरी चीज़ को ज़्यादा पसंद करते हैं, तो 'prefer [चीज़ 1] to [चीज़ 2]' का प्रयोग करते हैं। हिन्दी में हम 'चाय से ज़्यादा कॉफ़ी' कहते हैं, इसलिए कई लोग अंग्रेज़ी में '*prefer coffee than tea*' बोल देते हैं, जो बिल्कुल गलत है; सही शब्द 'to' है!

**English Explanation**:
In English, verbs expressing affection, aversion, and preference (like, love, enjoy, hate, dislike, don't mind) are complemented either by a direct noun or a verbal noun (gerund ending in '-ing'). Using a bare infinitive ('*I like cook*') is a major structural error. Furthermore, the verb 'prefer' takes the preposition 'to' (not 'than') when contrasting two choices: 'I prefer [A] to [B]'. When both elements are actions, both take the '-ing' form: 'She prefers walking to taking the bus.'

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Preference Verbs + Gerund (-ing)** [Core Syntax]
  Formula: Subject + [like / love / enjoy / hate / dislike] + [Verb-ING / Noun]. Example: 'I enjoy swimming.' (Never '*I enjoy swim*').
- **Rule 2: The 'Prefer X to Y' Comparative Rule** [Universal Grammar Standard]
  Formula: Subject + prefer(s) + [Noun/Gerund A] + TO + [Noun/Gerund B]. Never use 'than' with prefer. Correct: 'He prefers tea to coffee.'
- **Rule 3: Neutral Tolerance with 'Don't Mind'** [Conversational Politeness]
  'Don't mind + Verb-ING' indicates neutral acceptance (something is not a problem). Example: 'I don't mind waiting for ten minutes.'
- **Rule 4: Questions about Leisure and Hobbies** [Social Inquiries]
  Use 'What do you like doing in your free time?' or 'What are your hobbies?' followed by '-ing' responses.

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "degree": "Strong Affection (100%)",
    "formula": "Subject + love(s) + Verb-ING",
    "exampleEn": "She loves traveling to new places.",
    "meaningHi": "उसे नई जगहों की यात्रा करना बहुत पसंद है।"
  },
  {
    "degree": "Enjoyment / Like (75%)",
    "formula": "Subject + like(s) / enjoy(s) + Verb-ING",
    "exampleEn": "We enjoy cooking dinner together.",
    "meaningHi": "हमें साथ मिलकर रात का खाना बनाना अच्छा लगता है।"
  },
  {
    "degree": "Comparative Choice",
    "formula": "Subject + prefer(s) + [A] + to + [B]",
    "exampleEn": "I prefer reading books to watching TV.",
    "meaningHi": "मुझे टीवी देखने से ज़्यादा किताबें पढ़ना पसंद है।"
  },
  {
    "degree": "Neutral Tolerance (50%)",
    "formula": "Subject + don't / doesn't mind + Verb-ING",
    "exampleEn": "He doesn't mind working late.",
    "meaningHi": "उसे देर तक काम करने में कोई आपत्ति नहीं है।"
  },
  {
    "degree": "Strong Dislike (0%)",
    "formula": "Subject + hate(s) / can't stand + Verb-ING",
    "exampleEn": "They hate waiting in long lines.",
    "meaningHi": "उन्हें लंबी कतारों में इंतज़ार करना बिल्कुल नापसंद है।"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Using 'Than' with 'Prefer' (*'I prefer tea than coffee'*) and Dropping '-ing' (*'I like swim'*)
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'मुझे चाय की तुलना में कॉफ़ी पसंद है' में तुलनात्मक शब्द 'से' आता है, इसलिए शिक्षार्थी अंग्रेज़ी में 'than' लगा देते हैं (*I prefer coffee than tea*)। इसके अलावा क्रिया का मूल रूप बोल देते हैं (*I like swim*)।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*I prefer train than flight*' and '*He loves play football*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: In English, 'prefer' is a Latin comparative verb that strictly pairs with the preposition 'to'. Also, verbs of enjoyment require the activity to be framed as a nominal gerund (-ing).
- **Natural English Solution**: **Say 'I prefer trains to flights' and 'He loves playing football'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day14_vocab_001` | **Enjoy** | Verb | `/ɪnˈdʒɔɪ/` | आनंद लेना / पसंद करना | I enjoy listening to podcast episodes. <br> *मुझे पॉडकास्ट सुनने में आनंद आता है।* |
| `day14_vocab_002` | **Prefer** | Verb | `/prɪˈfɜːr/` | अधिक पसंद करना / प्राथमिकता देना | Do you prefer tea to coffee? <br> *क्या आप चाय के मुकाबले कॉफ़ी ज़्यादा पसंद करते हैं?* |
| `day14_vocab_003` | **Hate** | Verb | `/heɪt/` | नफ़रत करना / बिल्कुल नापसंद होना | She hates waking up early on Sundays. <br> *उसे रविवार को जल्दी उठना बिल्कुल पसंद नहीं है।* |
| `day14_vocab_004` | **Cook** | Verb | `/kʊk/` | खाना पकाना | He likes cooking Italian food at home. <br> *उसे घर पर इटैलियन खाना पकाना पसंद है।* |
| `day14_vocab_005` | **Travel** | Verb | `/ˈtræv.əl/` | यात्रा करना / घूमना | We love traveling during winter vacations. <br> *हमें सर्दियों की छुट्टियों में घूमना बहुत पसंद है।* |
| `day14_vocab_006` | **Paint** | Verb | `/peɪnt/` | चित्रकारी करना / पेंट करना | She enjoys painting landscapes in watercolor. <br> *उसे जलरंग से प्राकृतिक दृश्य चित्रित करना अच्छा लगता है।* |
| `day14_vocab_007` | **Read** | Verb | `/riːd/` | पढ़ना | I love reading historical novels. <br> *मुझे ऐतिहासिक उपन्यास पढ़ना बहुत पसंद है।* |
| `day14_vocab_008` | **Hobby** | Noun | `/ˈhɒb.i/` | शौक / रुचि | Gardening is my grandfather's favorite hobby. <br> *बागवानी मेरे दादाजी का सबसे पसंदीदा शौक है।* |
| `day14_vocab_009` | **Spare time** | Noun | `/speər taɪm/` | खाली समय / फुर्सत का समय | What do you do in your spare time? <br> *आप अपने खाली समय में क्या करते हैं?* |
| `day14_vocab_010` | **Relax** | Verb | `/rɪˈlæks/` | आराम करना / सुकून पाना | I like relaxing with a good book. <br> *मुझे एक अच्छी किताब के साथ आराम करना पसंद है।* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day14_phrase_001` [Social Inquiry]: **"What do you like doing in your free time?"** — *आप अपने खाली समय में क्या करना पसंद करते हैं?*
- `day14_phrase_002` [Stative Preference]: **"I prefer tea to coffee."** — *मुझे कॉफ़ी से ज़्यादा चाय पसंद है।*
- `day14_phrase_003` [Conversational Politeness]: **"I don't mind walking to work."** — *मुझे पैदल काम पर जाने में कोई आपत्ति नहीं है।*
- `day14_phrase_004` [Idiomatic Expression]: **"She is really keen on photography."** — *उसे फोटोग्राफी का बहुत शौक है।*
- `day14_phrase_005` [Social Conversation]: **"How do you usually spend your weekends?"** — *आप आमतौर पर अपना सप्ताहांत कैसे बिताते हैं?*
- `day14_phrase_006` [Strong Dislike]: **"I can't stand noisy places."** — *मुझे शोर-शराबे वाली जगहें बिल्कुल बर्दाश्त नहीं होतीं।*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day14_example_001`: **In my free time, I like playing badminton with my neighbors.**  
  *अपने खाली समय में मुझे पड़ोसियों के साथ बैडमिंटन खेलना पसंद है।*
- `day14_example_002`: **Pooja loves listening to classical Indian music while studying.**  
  *पूजा को पढ़ाई करते समय भारतीय शास्त्रीय संगीत सुनना बहुत पसंद है।*
- `day14_example_003`: **My brother prefers staying at home to going to crowded parties.**  
  *मेरा भाई भीड़-भाड़ वाली पार्टियों में जाने के मुकाबले घर पर रहना ज़्यादा पसंद करता है।*
- `day14_example_004`: **They don't enjoy watching horror movies at night.**  
  *उन्हें रात में डरावनी फ़िल्में देखना अच्छा नहीं लगता।*
- `day14_example_005`: **Do you enjoy cooking for your friends on weekends?**  
  *क्या आपको सप्ताहांत पर अपने दोस्तों के लिए खाना बनाना अच्छा लगता है?*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Stress on the second syllable of 'prefer' /prɪˈfɜːr/, Clean velar nasal /ɪŋ/ in '-ing' endings without a heavy /ɡ/, Vowel contrast: 'cook' /kʊk/ vs. 'food' /fuːd/
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do NOT pronounce 'prefer' as 'PREE-fer'. The primary stress is on the second syllable: pre-FER /prɪˈfɜːr/.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day14_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "I like reading books in my free time."
- `day14_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "She prefers swimming to running."
- `day14_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "What do you enjoy doing on Sundays?"
- `day14_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "I don't mind helping you with the project."

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day14_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `GERUND_PREFERENCES`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to express a hobby:
**Options**: `I enjoy to swim in the morning.` | `I enjoy swimming in the morning.` | `I enjoy swim in the morning.` | `I am enjoy swimming in the morning.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I enjoy swimming in the morning.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: The verb 'enjoy' must be followed directly by a gerund (-ing): 'enjoy swimming'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Enjoy' के बाद क्रिया का '-ing' रूप (swimming) आता है।

#### 2. `[day14_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PREFER_SYNTAX`)
**Prompt**: Choose the grammatically correct sentence using 'prefer':
**Options**: `I prefer tea than coffee.` | `I prefer tea to coffee.` | `I prefer tea over to coffee.` | `I prefer tea more than coffee.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I prefer tea to coffee.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: In standard English, 'prefer' strictly pairs with the preposition 'to'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Prefer' के साथ हमेशा 'to' आता है, 'than' कभी नहीं।

#### 3. `[day14_ex_003]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `NEGATION_PREFERENCES`)
**Prompt**: Select the correct sentence for 'उसे खाना पकाना पसंद नहीं है':
**Options**: `She doesn't like cooking.` | `She doesn't likes cook.` | `She don't like cooking.` | `She not like cooking.`
- **Correct Answer**: `She doesn't like cooking.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'She' requires 'doesn't like' followed by the gerund 'cooking'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'She' के साथ 'doesn't like cooking' सही है।

#### 4. `[day14_ex_004]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `GERUND_PREFERENCES`)
**Prompt**: Complete the sentence: 'My father hates ______ in long traffic jams.'
**Options**: `wait` | `waiting` | `waits` | `to waiting`
- **Correct Answer**: `waiting`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Hate' takes the gerund form 'waiting'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Hates' के बाद 'waiting' आएगा।

#### 5. `[day14_ex_005]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Which question correctly asks about someone's weekend activities?
**Options**: `What do you like doing on weekends?` | `What you like do on weekends?` | `What are you like do on weekends?` | `What do you like do on weekends?`
- **Correct Answer**: `What do you like doing on weekends?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Formula: What + do + you + like + doing + time phrase?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: What do you like doing on weekends?

**Prompt**: Rohan loves ______ (listen) to old Bollywood songs.
- **Correct Answer**: `listening`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use the gerund 'listening' after the preference verb 'loves'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Loves' के बाद 'listening' आएगा।

#### 7. `[day14_ex_007]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: We prefer traveling by train ______ (to / than) flying.
- **Correct Answer**: `to`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Prefer' takes the preposition 'to'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Prefer' के साथ 'to' आता है।

- **Correct Answer**: `closing`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Mind' is followed by the gerund 'closing'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Do you mind' के बाद क्रिया का '-ing' रूप (closing) आता है।
