**Prompt**: She enjoys ______ (paint) landscapes on Sunday mornings.
- **Correct Answer**: `painting`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Enjoys' requires the gerund 'painting'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Enjoys' के बाद 'painting' आएगा।

#### 10. `[day14_ex_010]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: They don't like ______ (wake) up early during holidays.
- **Correct Answer**: `waking`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use the gerund 'waking' after 'don't like'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Don't like' के बाद 'waking' आएगा।

#### 11. `[day14_ex_011]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'I prefer coffee than tea.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I prefer coffee to tea.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Replace 'than' with 'to' after 'prefer'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Prefer' के बाद 'than' हटाकर 'to' लिखें: 'I prefer coffee to tea.'

#### 12. `[day14_ex_012]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'He likes play football in the evening.'
- **Correct Answer**: `He likes playing football in the evening.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `He likes to play football in the evening.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use the gerund 'playing' after 'likes'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Likes' के बाद 'play' की जगह 'playing' आएगा।

#### 13. `[day14_ex_013]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'She enjoys to read historical books.'
- **Correct Answer**: `She enjoys reading historical books.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Enjoy' is always followed by a gerund (-ing), never an infinitive with 'to'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Enjoy' के साथ सीधे '-ing' (reading) आता है, 'to read' नहीं।

#### 14. `[day14_ex_014]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)

- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'to' between the two gerunds with 'prefer'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Prefer staying at home TO going out' सही रूप है।

- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `I enjoy reading books on weekends.`, `I love reading books on weekends.`

#### 22. `[day14_ex_022]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (medium | `TRANSLATION`)

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Two colleagues chatting over lunch in the office cafeteria on a Friday afternoon, sharing weekend plans and personal hobbies.

#### Dialogue (`day14_dialogue_001`):
- **Deepak**: "Friday is finally here! What do you like doing in your free time, Sarah?"  
  *(आखिरकार शुक्रवार आ गया! सारा, आप अपने खाली समय में क्या करना पसंद करती हैं?)*
- **Sarah**: "I love hiking in the hills and taking photographs. How about you?"  
  *(मुझे पहाड़ियों में पैदल घूमना (hiking) और तस्वीरें खींचना बहुत पसंद है। आप क्या करते हैं?)*
- **Deepak**: "I enjoy playing badminton, but I prefer indoor games to outdoor sports in the summer."  
  *(मुझे बैडमिंटन खेलना अच्छा लगता है, लेकिन गर्मियों में मुझे बाहर के खेलों से ज़्यादा इनडोर खेल पसंद हैं।)*
- **Sarah**: "That makes sense. Do you like watching movies at home as well?"  
  *(हाँ, यह बात सही है। क्या आपको घर पर फ़िल्में देखना भी पसंद है?)*
- **Deepak**: "Yes! I prefer watching documentaries to action movies. They are very informative."  
  *(हाँ! मुझे एक्शन फ़िल्मों के मुकाबले डॉक्यूमेंट्री देखना ज़्यादा पसंद है। वे बहुत ज्ञानवर्धक होती हैं।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Record a 4-sentence monologue describing your personal leisure habits: 1) What you love doing in your free time, 2) One activity you enjoy on weekends, 3) One comparison using 'I prefer [A] to [B]', and 4) One thing you hate doing.
- **HI**: अपने खाली समय के बारे में 4 वाक्यों का ऑडियो रिकॉर्ड करें: 1) जो आपको बहुत पसंद है, 2) जो आप सप्ताहांत पर करते हैं, 3) 'I prefer A to B' का एक वाक्य, 4) जो आपको बिल्कुल पसंद नहीं है।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day14_assess_001]` **Why is 'I prefer tea than coffee' grammatically incorrect?** ➔ *Because 'prefer' requires the preposition 'to', not the conjunction 'than' (I prefer tea TO coffee).* [Skill: Syntax Precision]
- `[day14_assess_002]` **What is the structural rule when combining 'enjoy' or 'like' with an action verb?** ➔ *The following action verb must take the gerund (-ing) form (e.g., 'enjoy swimming', 'like reading').* [Skill: Morphological Precision]
- `[day14_assess_003]` **What does 'I don't mind waiting' communicate socially?** ➔ *It communicates polite, neutral tolerance—that waiting causes no inconvenience.* [Skill: Pragmatic Competence]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `HOBBIES_PREFERENCES_GERUNDS_L1`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 15: Abilities, Skills & Polite Requests (Can / Can't & Modal Mechanics) {#day-15-day_15}
**शीर्षक**: योग्यता, क्षमताएं और विनम्र अनुरोध (Can / Can't तथा Modal क्रियाओं के नियम)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.1` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 02 (Polite Phrases), Day 08 (Present Simple Base Verbs)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Express general physical and mental abilities using the modal auxiliary 'can' + bare infinitive across all subjects.
  - **HI**: शारीरिक और मानसिक क्षमता बताने के लिए 'can + क्रिया का मूल रूप (V1)' का प्रयोग करना।
- **EN**: Form negative ability statements with 'can't' (/kænt/ or /kɑːnt/) and 'cannot' without adding '-s' or 'to'.
  - **HI**: अक्षमता व्यक्त करने के लिए 'can't' या 'cannot' का सही उपयोग करना (बिना 's' या 'to' जोड़े)।
- **EN**: Construct polite requests and permission inquiries using 'Can you...?' and the softer, more formal 'Could you please...?'
  - **HI**: विनम्र अनुरोध और अनुमति के लिए 'Can you...?' तथा अधिक औपचारिक 'Could you please...?' बोलना।
- **EN**: Eliminate classic Hindi interference patterns such as '*He cans drive*', '*I can to swim*', or '*I know to drive*'.
  - **HI**: हिन्दी से होने वाली गलतियाँ जैसे '*He cans drive*', '*I can to swim*' या '*I know to drive*' को पूरी तरह बंद करना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
हिन्दी में जब हम किसी काम को 'कर सकना' या अपनी क्षमता बताते हैं (जैसे: 'मैं तैर सकता हूँ', 'वह गाड़ी चला सकती है'), तो अंग्रेज़ी में Modal सहायक क्रिया 'Can' का प्रयोग होता है। Modal क्रियाओं के दो सबसे महत्वपूर्ण अटूट नियम हैं: 1) Can सभी कर्ताओं (I, You, He, She, It, We, They) के साथ हमेशा एक समान रहता है—इसमें कभी भी 's' या 'es' नहीं जुड़ता (जैसे: 'He can', कभी भी '*He cans*' नहीं होता)। 2) Can के बाद आने वाली मुख्य क्रिया हमेशा अपने मूल रूप (Bare Infinitive) में होती है—उससे पहले 'to' कभी नहीं लगता (जैसे: 'I can swim', कभी भी '*I can to swim*' नहीं होता)। इसके अलावा, जब हमें किसी से कोई मदद मांगनी हो, तो हम विनम्रता से 'Could you please...?' का प्रयोग करते हैं।

**English Explanation**:
The modal auxiliary 'CAN' expresses capability, possibility, permission, and requests. Modals are defective verbs with two non-negotiable syntactic rules: 1) Invariable Form: Modals never take third-person '-s' (e.g., 'He can', NEVER '*He cans*'). 2) Bare Infinitive Complement: Modals must be followed directly by a base verb without 'to' (e.g., 'She can drive', NEVER '*She can to drive*' or '*She can driving*'). For polite requests, 'Could you please + base verb' provides a softer, more professional register than 'Can you'.

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Invariable Modal Rule (No '-s' for 3rd Person)** [Universal Grammar Standard]
  Modal 'can' remains identical for all grammatical persons: I can, you can, he can, she can, we can, they can. Never add '-s'.
- **Rule 2: Bare Infinitive Rule (No 'to', No '-ing')** [Core Syntax]
  Formula: Subject + CAN/CAN'T + [Base Verb (V1)]. Never insert 'to' (e.g., 'I can speak', not '*I can to speak*').
- **Rule 3: The Single-Word Spelling of 'Cannot'** [Orthographic Standard]
  The formal full negative of 'can' is written as ONE combined word: 'cannot' (not '*can not*' unless 'not' belongs to another clause). The standard spoken contraction is 'can't'.
- **Rule 4: Polite Request Hierarchy ('Can' vs. 'Could')** [Pragmatic Politeness]
  'Can you help me?' is friendly and direct. 'Could you please help me?' adds social distance and elevated politeness, ideal for professional or formal interactions.

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "functionType": "Affirmative Ability",
    "formula": "Subject + can + Base Verb (V1)",
    "exampleEn": "He can speak three languages.",
    "meaningHi": "वह तीन भाषाएं बोल सकता है।"
  },
  {
    "functionType": "Negative Inability",
    "formula": "Subject + can't (cannot) + Base Verb",
    "exampleEn": "I can't swim in deep water.",
    "meaningHi": "मैं गहरे पानी में नहीं तैर सकता।"
  },
  {
    "functionType": "Ability Question",
    "formula": "Can + Subject + Base Verb + ...?",
    "exampleEn": "Can you drive a manual car?",
    "meaningHi": "क्या आप मैनुअल कार चला सकते हैं?"
  },
  {
    "functionType": "Polite Request",
    "formula": "Could you + [please] + Base Verb + ...?",
    "exampleEn": "Could you please repeat that?",
    "meaningHi": "क्या आप कृपया वह दोबारा दोहरा सकते हैं?"
  },
  {
    "functionType": "Asking Permission",
    "formula": "Can / May I + Base Verb + ...?",
    "exampleEn": "Can I use your charger?",
    "meaningHi": "क्या मैं आपका चार्जर इस्तेमाल कर सकता हूँ?"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Adding 'to' (*'I can to do it'*), adding '-s' (*'He cans'*), and '*I know to drive*'
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'मुझे गाड़ी चलाना आता है' का शाब्दिक अनुवाद शिक्षार्थी '*I know to drive*' कर देते हैं, या 'can' के बाद 'to' लगा देते हैं (*I can to drive*)।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*I can to speak English*', '*He cans help*', or '*I know to cook*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: In English, 'can' requires the bare verb directly: 'I can speak'. If using the verb 'know', the required English idiom is 'know how to': 'I know HOW TO drive'.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Say 'I can drive' or 'I know how to drive'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day15_vocab_001` | **Swim** | Verb | `/swɪm/` | तैरना | Can you swim across the river? <br> *क्या आप नदी के पार तैर सकते हैं?* |
| `day15_vocab_002` | **Drive** | Verb | `/draɪv/` | गाड़ी चलाना | She can drive both automatic and manual cars. <br> *वह ऑटोमैटिक और मैनुअल दोनों कारें चला सकती है।* |
| `day15_vocab_003` | **Type** | Verb | `/taɪp/` | टाइप करना / कंप्यूटर पर लिखना | He can type sixty words per minute. <br> *वह प्रति मिनट साठ शब्द टाइप कर सकता है।* |
| `day15_vocab_004` | **Fix** | Verb | `/fɪks/` | ठीक करना / मरम्मत करना | Can you fix this leaking tap? <br> *क्या आप इस टपकते नल को ठीक कर सकते हैं?* |
| `day15_vocab_005` | **Solve** | Verb | `/sɒlv/` | हल करना | They can solve complex math problems quickly. <br> *वे गणित की कठिन समस्याएं जल्दी हल कर सकते हैं।* |
| `day15_vocab_006` | **Sing** | Verb | `/sɪŋ/` | गाना गाना | My sister can sing classical melodies. <br> *मेरी बहन शास्त्रीय गीत गा सकती है।* |
| `day15_vocab_007` | **Draw** | Verb | `/drɔː/` | चित्र बनाना / रेखाचित्र खींचना | He can draw realistic portraits. <br> *वह जीवंत चित्र बना सकता है।* |
| `day15_vocab_008` | **Hear** | Verb | `/hɪər/` | सुनना / सुनाई देना | I can't hear your voice clearly. <br> *मुझे आपकी आवाज़ साफ़ सुनाई नहीं दे रही है।* |
| `day15_vocab_009` | **Explain** | Verb | `/ɪkˈspleɪn/` | समझाना / स्पष्ट करना | Could you explain this step once more? <br> *क्या आप कृपया यह चरण एक बार और समझा सकते हैं?* |
| `day15_vocab_010` | **Repeat** | Verb | `/rɪˈpiːt/` | दोहराना | Could you please repeat your email address? <br> *क्या आप कृपया अपना ईमेल पता दोबारा बता सकते हैं?* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day15_phrase_001` [Polite Request]: **"Could you please help me with this?"** — *क्या आप कृपया इसमें मेरी मदद कर सकते हैं?*
- `day15_phrase_002` [Polite Clarification]: **"Could you repeat that, please?"** — *क्या आप कृपया वह दोबारा दोहरा सकते हैं?*
- `day15_phrase_003` [Phone / Video Call Formula]: **"I can't hear you clearly."** — *मुझे आपकी आवाज़ साफ़ नहीं आ रही है।*
- `day15_phrase_004` [Asking Permission]: **"Can I open the window?"** — *क्या मैं खिड़की खोल सकता हूँ?*
- `day15_phrase_005` [Phone / Customer Service]: **"Could you hold on for a second?"** — *क्या आप कृपया एक पल के लिए रुक सकते हैं?*
- `day15_phrase_006` [Skill Competence]: **"She can speak fluent English."** — *वह धाराप्रवाह अंग्रेज़ी बोल सकती है।*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day15_example_001`: **Aman can play the guitar very well.**  
  *अमन बहुत अच्छी तरह गिटार बजा सकता है।*
- `day15_example_002`: **I can't come to the office tomorrow because I am unwell.**  
  *मैं कल दफ़्तर नहीं आ सकता क्योंकि मेरी तबीयत ठीक नहीं है।*
- `day15_example_003`: **Can you lend me your pen for a minute?**  
  *क्या आप मुझे एक मिनट के लिए अपना पेन दे सकते हैं?*
- `day15_example_004`: **Could you please speak a little louder?**  
  *क्या आप कृपया थोड़ा ज़ोर से बोल सकते हैं?*
- `day15_example_005`: **My grandmother cannot walk long distances without support.**  
  *मेरी दादी सहारे के बिना लंबी दूरी नहीं चल सकती हैं।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Weak, unstressed /kən/ in affirmative statements: 'I can do it' -> /aɪ kən ˈduː ɪt/, Strong, stressed /kænt/ (US) or /kɑːnt/ (UK) in negative statements: 'I can't do it', Soft /d/ in 'could' /kʊd/ with silent 'l'
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do NOT pronounce the 'l' in 'could' (it is /kʊd/, never 'coold'). In affirmative sentences, do not over-stress 'can'—keep it short (/kən/).
#### Audio Drills:
- `day15_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "I can meet you at five o'clock. (/kən/)"
- `day15_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "I can't hear what you are saying. (/kænt/ or /kɑːnt/)"
- `day15_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "Could you please pass the water bottle?"
- `day15_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "Can you speak a little slower?"

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day15_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `MODAL_RULES`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to express ability:
**Options**: `He can to swim.` | `He cans swim.` | `He can swim.` | `He can swimming.`
- **Correct Answer**: `He can swim.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Modals take neither '-s' nor 'to'. Use 'can' + base verb 'swim'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Can' के साथ 's' या 'to' नहीं लगता; 'He can swim' सही है।

#### 2. `[day15_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `POLITE_REQUESTS`)
**Prompt**: Which sentence is the MOST polite request in a business context?
**Options**: `You help me now!` | `Can you help me?` | `Could you please help me with this?` | `Help me with this please.`
- **Correct Answer**: `Could you please help me with this?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Could you please...' is the standard polite formula in professional registers.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Could you please help me with this?' सबसे विनम्र और पेशेवर रूप है।

#### 3. `[day15_ex_003]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `MODAL_SPELLING`)
**Prompt**: Select the correct negative spelling and grammar for 'cannot':
**Options**: `She can not to come.` | `She cannot come.` | `She cann't come.` | `She cannots come.`
- **Correct Answer**: `She cannot come.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Cannot' is written as a single word followed by the bare base verb 'come'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Cannot' एक साथ लिखा जाता है और इसके बाद मूल क्रिया 'come' आती है।

#### 4. `[day15_ex_004]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `SKILL_EXPRESSION`)
**Prompt**: How do you say 'मुझे कार चलाना आता है' in natural English?
**Options**: `I know to drive a car.` | `I can drive a car.` | `I can to drive a car.` | `I know driving car.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I can drive a car.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'I can drive a car' (or 'I know how to drive') is the natural equivalent of knowing a practical skill.
- **Explanation (HI)**: गाड़ी चलाने की क्षमता के लिए 'I can drive a car' या 'I know how to drive' बोला जाता है।

#### 5. `[day15_ex_005]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PRONUNCIATION`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct pronunciation of 'could':
**Options**: `/kuːld/ (with 'l' pronounced)` | `/kʊd/ (with silent 'l')` | `/kəʊld/ (like cold)` | `/kʌd/ (like cud)`
- **Correct Answer**: `/kʊd/ (with silent 'l')`
- **Explanation (EN)**: The 'l' in 'could', 'would', and 'should' is completely silent: /kʊd/.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Could' में 'l' silent होता है, इसका उच्चारण /kʊd/ (कुड) है।

#### 6. `[day15_ex_006]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: She ______ (can / cans) speak three foreign languages fluently.
- **Correct Answer**: `can`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Modals never take '-s', even with third-person subjects.
- **Explanation (HI)**: Modal क्रियाओं में कभी 's' नहीं जुड़ता, इसलिए 'can' आएगा।

#### 7. `[day15_ex_007]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: Could you please ______ (repeat / to repeat) the phone number?
- **Correct Answer**: `repeat`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Could' is followed directly by the bare verb 'repeat' without 'to'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Could' के बाद बिना 'to' के मूल क्रिया 'repeat' आएगी।

#### 8. `[day15_ex_008]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: I am sorry, but I ______ (can't / not can) attend the meeting today.
- **Correct Answer**: `can't`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `cannot`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'can't' or 'cannot' for negative ability/availability.
- **Explanation (HI)**: नकारात्मक के लिए 'can't' का प्रयोग करें।

#### 9. `[day15_ex_009]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: ______ (Can / Do) you play the piano?
- **Correct Answer**: `Can`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'Can you play...' to ask about learned musical skill.
- **Explanation (HI)**: संगीत वाद्ययंत्र बजाने की क्षमता पूछने के लिए 'Can you play...?' आता है।

#### 10. `[day15_ex_010]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: The mechanic ______ (can / is can) repair this engine in two hours.
- **Correct Answer**: `can`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Do not combine the BE verb with modal 'can'. Use 'can repair'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Is can' गलत है; सीधे 'can repair' लिखें।

#### 11. `[day15_ex_011]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'My brother cans speak German.'
- **Correct Answer**: `My brother can speak German.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Remove '-s' from 'cans'. Modals are invariant.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Cans' में से 's' हटाएं: 'My brother can speak German.'

#### 12. `[day15_ex_012]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'I can to swim very fast.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I can swim very fast.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Remove 'to' after the modal 'can'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Can' के बाद 'to' नहीं लगता: 'I can swim very fast.'

#### 13. `[day15_ex_013]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'Could you please to send me the document?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Could you please send me the document?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Remove 'to' after 'could you please'. Use bare infinitive 'send'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Could you please' के बाद 'to' हटाकर सीधे 'send' लिखें।

#### 14. `[day15_ex_014]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'I don't can hear you clearly.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I can't hear you clearly.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `I cannot hear you clearly.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Never use 'don't can'. The negative of 'can' is 'can't' or 'cannot'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Don't can' नहीं होता, 'can't hear' या 'cannot hear' लिखें।

#### 15. `[day15_ex_015]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'She knows to cook delicious biryani.'
- **Correct Answer**: `She knows how to cook delicious biryani.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `She can cook delicious biryani.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Say 'knows HOW TO cook' or 'can cook', never 'knows to cook'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Knows to cook' की जगह 'knows how to cook' या 'can cook' लिखें।

- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: Could you please repeat that?

- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Could you hold on for a moment' is used when asking someone to wait briefly.

**Prompt**: Complete the interaction: Tech Support: 'Are you able to see your screen now?' User: 'No, I ______ (can / not / see) anything on the monitor.'
- **Correct Answer**: `can't see`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `cannot see`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'can't see' or 'cannot see'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Can't see' सही नकारात्मक रूप है।

#### 33. `[day15_ex_033]` [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (easy | `SPEAKING`)
**Prompt**: Speak aloud: Make a polite request to someone to open a door using 'Could you please...?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Could you please open the door?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Practice saying 'Could you please open the door?' with gentle rising intonation.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Could you please open the door?' बोलकर अभ्यास करें।

#### 34. `[day15_ex_034]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Why is 'He cans drive' grammatically wrong?
- **Correct Answer**: `Because modal verbs like 'can' are invariant and never take an '-s' suffix.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Modals do not conjugate for third-person singular.
- **Explanation (HI)**: Modal क्रियाओं ('can') में कभी भी 's' नहीं लगता।

#### 35. `[day15_ex_035]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: What is the difference in politeness between 'Can you help me?' and 'Could you help me?'
- **Correct Answer**: `'Could you help me?' is softer, more tentative, and more polite/formal than 'Can you help me?'.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Could' provides higher pragmatic deference.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Could you help me?' अधिक विनम्र और औपचारिक होता है।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: A virtual team meeting on Zoom where team members troubleshoot audio issues and delegate technical tasks.

#### Dialogue (`day15_dialogue_001`):
- **Naveen**: "Good morning team. Can everyone hear me clearly?"  
  *(शुभ प्रभात टीम। क्या हर कोई मुझे साफ़ सुन पा रहा है?)*
- **Priya**: "Good morning Naveen! Your voice is a bit quiet. Could you please turn up your microphone volume?"  
  *(शुभ प्रभात नवीन! आपकी आवाज़ थोड़ी धीमी है। क्या आप कृपया अपने माइक की आवाज़ बढ़ा सकते हैं?)*
- **Naveen**: "Sure, let me adjust it. Is this better now?"  
  *(ज़रूर, मैं इसे ठीक करता हूँ। क्या अब यह बेहतर है?)*
- **Priya**: "Yes, perfectly clear now! Who can share the presentation slides on screen?"  
  *(हाँ, अब बिल्कुल साफ़ है! स्क्रीन पर प्रेजेंटेशन स्लाइड कौन शेयर कर सकता है?)*
- **Rahul**: "I can share the deck right away. Could you please give me host permissions, Naveen?"  
  *(मैं अभी प्रेजेंटेशन शेयर कर सकता हूँ। नवीन, क्या आप कृपया मुझे होस्ट की अनुमति दे सकते हैं?)*
- **Naveen**: "Done! You can present now."  
  *(हो गया! अब आप प्रेजेंट कर सकते हैं।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Record yourself making 3 polite requests in a work or study scenario: 1) Asking someone to repeat an explanation, 2) Asking someone to speak slower, and 3) Asking someone for assistance with a technical file.
- **HI**: दफ़्तर या पढ़ाई की स्थिति में 3 विनम्र अनुरोध बोलने का ऑडियो रिकॉर्ड करें: 1) बात दोहराने के लिए, 2) धीरे बोलने के लिए, और 3) फ़ाइल में मदद के लिए।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day15_assess_001]` **Why is 'I can to swim' ungrammatical?** ➔ *Because modal auxiliaries must be followed by a bare infinitive (without 'to').* [Skill: Syntax Precision]
- `[day15_assess_002]` **How does the vowel sound of 'can' change between affirmative and negative sentences in connected speech?** ➔ *In affirmative statements, 'can' is reduced to the weak schwa /kən/; in negative statements, 'can't' is stressed with a full open vowel /kænt/ or /kɑːnt/.* [Skill: Phonological Competence]
- `[day15_assess_003]` **How is 'cannot' correctly punctuated in formal writing?** ➔ *It is written as a single, combined word: 'cannot'.* [Skill: Orthographic Precision]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `MODALS_CAN_COULD_ABILITIES_L1`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 16: Home & Rooms in the House (There is / There are & Spatial Existence) {#day-16-day_16}
**शीर्षक**: घर, कमरे और मौजूदगी (There is / There are तथा स्थानिक अस्तित्व)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.1` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 05 (Present BE), Day 13 (Some / Any & Quantifiers)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Describe the layout of houses and apartments accurately using existential 'There is' (singular/uncountable) and 'There are' (plural).
  - **HI**: घर और कमरों की बनावट बताने के लिए 'There is' (एकवचन) और 'There are' (बहुवचन) का सही प्रयोग करना।
- **EN**: Form negative statements about missing features ('There isn't a balcony', 'There aren't any chairs').
  - **HI**: नकारात्मक वाक्य बनाना (जैसे: 'There isn't a balcony', 'There aren't any chairs')।
- **EN**: Construct inquiries about accommodations ('Is there a lift?', 'Are there any windows?', 'How many rooms are there?').
  - **HI**: घर के बारे में प्रश्न पूछना (जैसे: 'Is there a lift?', 'How many bedrooms are there?')।
- **EN**: Eliminate classic Hindi-English errors such as using '*It is a balcony in my house*' or '*There have three rooms*'.
  - **HI**: हिन्दी से सीधे अनुवाद की गलतियाँ जैसे '*It is a balcony in my house*' या '*There have three rooms*' को पूरी तरह बंद करना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
जब हम किसी जगह पर किसी चीज़ की 'मौजूदगी' या 'होने' की बात करते हैं (जैसे: 'घर में तीन कमरे हैं', 'रसोई में एक खिड़की है'), तो अंग्रेज़ी में वाक्य की शुरुआत 'There is' या 'There are' से होती है। जब एकवचन वस्तु या अगणनीय संज्ञा हो, तो 'There is' (संक्षिप्त: There's) का प्रयोग होता है (जैसे: 'There is a large bedroom')। जब एक से अधिक वस्तुएं हों, तो 'There are' का प्रयोग होता है (जैसे: 'There are two bathrooms')। अक्सर भारतीय शिक्षार्थी हिन्दी के 'घर में तीन कमरे हैं' को देखकर '*There have three rooms*' या '*In my house is three rooms*' बोल देते हैं, जो गलत है। हमेशा 'There is / There are' का ढांचा प्रयोग करें।

**English Explanation**:
In English, existential statements that introduce the presence, existence, or availability of something in a location require the dummy subject construction 'There is / There are'. Use 'There is' (or contraction 'There's') before singular countable nouns ('There is a garden') and uncountable mass nouns ('There is some space'). Use 'There are' before plural count nouns ('There are four chairs'). For questions, invert the word order: 'Is there...?' and 'Are there...?' To ask about quantity, use 'How many [Plural Noun] are there?'.

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Existential Singular vs. Plural Agreement** [Core Syntax]
  Use 'There is' (+ a/an/one) with singular count nouns and uncountable nouns. Use 'There are' with plural count nouns.
- **Rule 2: Negative Existence Patterns** [Universal Grammar Standard]
  Singular: 'There isn't a [noun]' / 'There is no [noun]'. Plural: 'There aren't any [nouns]' / 'There are no [nouns]'.
- **Rule 3: Interrogative Inversion & Quantity Questions** [Inquiry Structure]
  Yes/No: 'Is there a [singular]?' / 'Are there any [plurals]?' | Quantity: 'How many [Plural Noun] are there [in location]?'
- **Rule 4: There Is vs. It Is Distinction** [Semantic Precision]
  Use 'There is' to state existence in a place ('There is a lamp on the table'). Use 'It is' to identify or describe a specific entity ('What is that? It is a lamp'). Never say '*It is a kitchen in my flat*'.

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "structure": "Singular Affirmative",
    "formula": "There is (There's) + a/an + Singular Noun + [Location]",
    "exampleEn": "There is a balcony in the bedroom.",
    "meaningHi": "बेडरूम में एक बालकनी है।"
  },
  {
    "structure": "Plural Affirmative",
    "formula": "There are + [Number / some / many] + Plural Noun + [Location]",
    "exampleEn": "There are three rooms in this flat.",
    "meaningHi": "इस फ्लैट में तीन कमरे हैं।"
  },
  {
    "structure": "Singular Negative",
    "formula": "There isn't + a/an + Singular Noun",
    "exampleEn": "There isn't a garage in this house.",
    "meaningHi": "इस घर में कोई गैराज नहीं है।"
  },
  {
    "structure": "Plural Negative",
    "formula": "There aren't + any + Plural Nouns",
    "exampleEn": "There aren't any chairs in the living room.",
    "meaningHi": "लिविंग रूम में कोई कुर्सियाँ नहीं हैं।"
  },
  {
    "structure": "Quantity Question",
    "formula": "How many + [Plural Noun] + are there + [Location]?",
    "exampleEn": "How many bedrooms are there?",
    "meaningHi": "वहाँ कितने बेडरूम हैं?"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Using '*There have*' or '*It is a balcony in my house*' for Existence
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'घर में बालकनी है' को देखकर शिक्षार्थी अक्सर 'In my house has balcony' या 'There have three rooms' बोल देते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*There have two bedrooms in my flat*' or '*It is a big garden in the back*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: 'There have' is grammatically impossible for spatial existence. 'Have' requires an active possessor subject ('The house HAS three rooms' or 'THERE ARE three rooms'). 'It is' cannot introduce location-based existence.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Say 'There are two bedrooms in my flat' or 'There is a big garden in the back'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day16_vocab_001` | **Bedroom** | Noun | `/ˈbed.ruːm/` | शयनकक्ष / बेडरूम | There are two bedrooms in our new apartment. <br> *हमारे नए अपार्टमेंट में दो बेडरूम हैं।* |
| `day16_vocab_002` | **Living room** | Noun | `/ˈlɪv.ɪŋ ˌruːm/` | बैठक / लिविंग रूम | The living room has a large sofa and a TV. <br> *लिविंग रूम में एक बड़ा सोफा और एक टीवी है।* |
| `day16_vocab_003` | **Kitchen** | Noun | `/ˈkɪtʃ.ən/` | रसोईघर / किचन | There is a modern kitchen with a gas stove. <br> *गैस चूल्हे के साथ एक आधुनिक रसोईघर है।* |
| `day16_vocab_004` | **Bathroom** | Noun | `/ˈbɑːθ.ruːm/` | स्नानघर / बाथरूम | Is there an attached bathroom in this room? <br> *क्या इस कमरे में अटैच्ड बाथरूम है?* |
| `day16_vocab_005` | **Balcony** | Noun | `/ˈbæl.kə.ni/` | बालकनी / छज्जा | There is a beautiful view from the balcony. <br> *बालकनी से एक सुंदर दृश्य दिखता है।* |
| `day16_vocab_006` | **Garden** | Noun | `/ˈɡɑː.dən/` | बगीचा / उद्यान | There is a small flower garden in front of the house. <br> *घर के सामने एक छोटा फूलों का बगीचा है।* |
| `day16_vocab_007` | **Garage** | Noun | `/ˈɡær.ɑːʒ/` | गैराज / गाड़ी खड़ी करने की जगह | There is a covered garage for two cars. <br> *दो कारों के लिए एक ढका हुआ गैराज है।* |
| `day16_vocab_008` | **Hallway** | Noun | `/ˈhɔːl.weɪ/` | गलियारा / हॉलवे | There are several family photos in the hallway. <br> *गलियारे में कई पारिवारिक तस्वीरें लगी हैं।* |
| `day16_vocab_009` | **Window** | Noun | `/ˈwɪn.dəʊ/` | खिड़की | There are two large windows for ventilation. <br> *हवा के आने-जाने के लिए दो बड़ी खिड़कियाँ हैं।* |
| `day16_vocab_010` | **Floor** | Noun | `/flɔːr/` | मंजिल / फ़र्श | We live on the third floor. <br> *हम तीसरी मंजिल पर रहते हैं।* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day16_phrase_001` [Real Estate / Inquiry]: **"How many rooms are there?"** — *वहाँ कितने कमरे हैं?*
- `day16_phrase_002` [Accommodation Inquiry]: **"Is there an elevator / lift in the building?"** — *क्या इमारत में लिफ्ट है?*
- `day16_phrase_003` [Description]: **"There is plenty of natural light."** — *वहाँ भरपूर प्राकृतिक रोशनी आती है।*
- `day16_phrase_004` [Description]: **"There isn't enough storage space."** — *सामान रखने के लिए पर्याप्त जगह नहीं है।*
- `day16_phrase_005` [Inquiry]: **"Are there any parking spaces available?"** — *क्या कोई पार्किंग की जगह उपलब्ध है?*
- `day16_phrase_006` [Atmosphere Description]: **"It feels very cozy and bright."** — *यह बहुत आरामदायक और उजालेदार लगता है।*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day16_example_001`: **There is a spacious living room and two bedrooms in our flat.**  
  *हमारे फ्लैट में एक बड़ा लिविंग रूम और दो बेडरूम हैं।*
- `day16_example_002`: **There are three windows in the dining area.**  
  *डाइनिंग एरिया में तीन खिड़कियाँ हैं।*
- `day16_example_003`: **Is there a balcony attached to the master bedroom? — Yes, there is.**  
  *क्या मास्टर बेडरूम से जुड़ी कोई बालकनी है? — हाँ, है।*
- `day16_example_004`: **There aren't any noisy neighbors on our floor.**  
  *हमारी मंजिल पर कोई शोर मचाने वाले पड़ोसी नहीं हैं।*
- `day16_example_005`: **How many bathrooms are there in this house? — There are two.**  
  *इस घर में कितने बाथरूम हैं? — दो हैं।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Voiced dental fricative /ð/ in 'there' /ðeər/, Smooth contraction /ðeərz/ for 'there is', Vowel linking: 'There are' -> /ˈðeər.ɑːr/
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do NOT pronounce 'there' with a hard Hindi /d̪/ ('dair'). Touch the tip of your tongue gently between your teeth to produce voiced /ð/.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day16_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "There is a big kitchen on the ground floor."
- `day16_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "There are two bedrooms upstairs."
- `day16_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "Is there a lift in this apartment building?"
- `day16_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "How many rooms are there in total?"

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day16_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `EXISTENTIAL_THERE`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to say 'इस फ्लैट में तीन कमरे हैं':
**Options**: `There have three rooms in this flat.` | `There are three rooms in this flat.` | `There is three rooms in this flat.` | `It is three rooms in this flat.`
- **Correct Answer**: `There are three rooms in this flat.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Plural subject 'three rooms' requires 'There are'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: बहुवचन 'three rooms' के साथ 'There are' आता है।

#### 2. `[day16_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `EXISTENTIAL_THERE`)
**Prompt**: Select the correct sentence for 'घर के सामने एक बगीचा है':
**Options**: `There is a garden in front of the house.` | `There are a garden in front of the house.` | `In front of house has a garden.` | `It is a garden in front of house.`
- **Correct Answer**: `There is a garden in front of the house.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Singular count noun 'a garden' takes 'There is'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: एकवचन 'a garden' के साथ 'There is' का प्रयोग होता है।

#### 3. `[day16_ex_003]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `QUANTITY_QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct question to ask about the number of bathrooms:
**Options**: `How many bathrooms is there?` | `How many bathrooms are there?` | `How much bathrooms are there?` | `How many bathroom is there?`
- **Correct Answer**: `How many bathrooms are there?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'How many' requires a plural noun (bathrooms) + 'are there'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'How many bathrooms are there?' सही प्रश्न रचना है।

#### 4. `[day16_ex_004]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `NEGATION`)
**Prompt**: Complete the negative sentence: 'There ______ any chairs in the empty room.'
**Options**: `isn't` | `aren't` | `don't` | `not are`
- **Correct Answer**: `aren't`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Chairs' is plural, so use 'aren't any'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Chairs' बहुवचन है, इसलिए 'aren't' आएगा।

#### 5. `[day16_ex_005]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `QUESTIONS`)
**Prompt**: Which question is grammatically CORRECT?
**Options**: `Is there a garage attached to the house?` | `Are there a garage attached to the house?` | `Does there a garage attached?` | `Has there a garage attached?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Is there a garage attached to the house?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'Is there a + singular noun' to ask about existence.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Is there a garage...?' सही रूप है।

#### 6. `[day16_ex_006]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: There ______ (is / are) a large dining table in the kitchen.
- **Correct Answer**: `is`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'A large dining table' is singular, so use 'is'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: एकवचन वस्तु के लिए 'is' का प्रयोग करें।

#### 7. `[day16_ex_007]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: There ______ (is / are) four windows in the living room.
- **Correct Answer**: `are`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Four windows' is plural, requiring 'are'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: चार खिड़कियों (बहुवचन) के लिए 'are' आएगा।

#### 8. `[day16_ex_008]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: ______ (Is / Are) there an elevator in this apartment block?
- **Correct Answer**: `Is`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'An elevator' is singular, so start with 'Is'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'An elevator' एकवचन है, इसलिए 'Is' से प्रश्न शुरू होगा।

#### 9. `[day16_ex_009]` [FILL_BLANK] (medium | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: There ______ (isn't / aren't) any hot water in the bathroom right now.
- **Correct Answer**: `isn't`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Water' is uncountable, so it takes the singular negative 'isn't'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Water' अगणनीय (Uncountable) है, इसलिए 'isn't' आएगा।

#### 10. `[day16_ex_010]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: How many balconies ______ (is / are) there in your flat?
- **Correct Answer**: `are`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Balconies' is plural, so use 'are there'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Balconies' बहुवचन है, इसलिए 'are' आएगा।

#### 11. `[day16_ex_011]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'There have two bedrooms in my house.'
- **Correct Answer**: `There are two bedrooms in my house.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'There are' instead of 'There have' for spatial presence.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'There have' की जगह 'There are' का प्रयोग करें।

#### 12. `[day16_ex_012]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'It is a small balcony on the second floor.'
- **Correct Answer**: `There is a small balcony on the second floor.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use existential 'There is' to state that a balcony exists in that location.
- **Explanation (HI)**: मौजूदगी बताने के लिए 'It is' नहीं, 'There is' का प्रयोग करें।

#### 13. `[day16_ex_013]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'There aren't some chairs in the kitchen.'
- **Correct Answer**: `There aren't any chairs in the kitchen.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `There are no chairs in the kitchen.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: In negative existence statements, change 'some' to 'any'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: नकारात्मक वाक्य में 'some' की जगह 'any' का प्रयोग करें।

#### 14. `[day16_ex_014]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'How many room are there in your flat?'
- **Correct Answer**: `How many rooms are there in your flat?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'How many' must be followed by the plural noun 'rooms'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'How many' के बाद संज्ञा का बहुवचन 'rooms' आता है।

#### 15. `[day16_ex_015]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)

- **Correct Answer**: `Are there any balconies in this flat?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Balconies' is plural, requiring 'Are there'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: बहुवचन 'balconies' के लिए 'Are there' आएगा।

- **Correct Answer**: `There is a garden behind the house`

- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: There is a garden behind the house.

- **Explanation (EN)**: There isn't + a garage + in this building.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: There isn't a garage in this building.

**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a question:

**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'हमारे फ्लैट में दो बेडरूम और एक बालकनी है।'
- **Correct Answer**: `There are two bedrooms and a balcony in our flat.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Our flat has two bedrooms and a balcony.`, `There are two bedrooms and one balcony in our flat.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'There are two bedrooms and a balcony in our flat' is standard English.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'There are two bedrooms and a balcony in our flat.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 22. `[day16_ex_022]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'क्या इस इमारत में कोई लिफ्ट है?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Is there a lift in this building?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Is there an elevator in this building?`, `Is there an elevator in the building?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Is there a lift in this building?' is the standard inquiry.

#### 23. `[day16_ex_023]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)

- **Explanation (EN)**: 'How many rooms are there in your new house?' follows proper question structure.

#### 26. `[day16_ex_026]` [TRANSLATION_EN_HI] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)

- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `मकान के पीछे एक छोटा सा बगीचा है।`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'There is a small garden' translates to 'एक छोटा बगीचा है'.

#### 27. `[day16_ex_027]` [TRANSLATION_EN_HI] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)

- **Correct Answer**: `क्या कोई पार्किंग की जगह उपलब्ध है?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `क्या पार्किंग की जगहें उपलब्ध हैं?`

#### 33. `[day16_ex_033]` [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (easy | `SPEAKING`)
**Prompt**: Speak aloud: Describe your own living space in 2 sentences using 'There is a...' and 'There are [number]...'.
- **Correct Answer**: `There is a [singular item/room]. There are [plural rooms/items].`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Practice transitioning between singular 'There is' and plural 'There are'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: अपने घर के बारे में 'There is' और 'There are' का प्रयोग करके 2 वाक्य बोलें।

#### 34. `[day16_ex_034]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: What is the difference between 'There is a sofa' and 'It is a sofa'?
- **Correct Answer**: `'There is a sofa' introduces the existence of a sofa in a location; 'It is a sofa' identifies what an object is.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'There is' denotes spatial existence; 'It is' denotes identification/description.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'There is' मौजूदगी बताता है; 'It is' किसी वस्तु की पहचान बताता है।

#### 35. `[day16_ex_035]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Why can you not say 'There have three rooms'?
- **Correct Answer**: `'There' requires the BE verb (is/are) for existence, not 'have'.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Spatial presence is expressed exclusively with 'There is / There are'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: मौजूदगी के लिए 'There are' आता है, 'There have' व्याकरण की दृष्टि से अमान्य है।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Viewing an apartment for rent with a property manager in a new residential tower.

#### Dialogue (`day16_dialogue_001`):
- **Aditi**: "Good afternoon! Thank you for showing me the flat. How many bedrooms are there?"  
  *(शुभ दोपहर! मुझे फ्लैट दिखाने के लिए धन्यवाद। यहाँ कितने बेडरूम हैं?)*
- **Agent**: "Good afternoon, Aditi! There are two large bedrooms and two attached bathrooms."  
  *(शुभ दोपहर, अदिति! यहाँ दो बड़े बेडरूम और दो अटैच्ड बाथरूम हैं।)*
- **Aditi**: "That's great. Is there a balcony in the living room?"  
  *(यह तो बहुत अच्छा है। क्या लिविंग रूम में कोई बालकनी है?)*
- **Agent**: "Yes, there is a lovely east-facing balcony with lots of morning sunlight."  
  *(हाँ, पूर्व दिशा की ओर एक सुंदर बालकनी है जहाँ सुबह की भरपूर धूप आती है।)*
- **Aditi**: "Wonderful. Are there any dedicated parking spaces in the basement?"  
  *(बहुत बढ़िया। क्या बेसमेंट में कोई तय पार्किंग की जगह है?)*
- **Agent**: "Yes, there is one reserved parking slot for this apartment."  
  *(हाँ, इस अपार्टमेंट के लिए एक आरक्षित पार्किंग स्लॉट है।)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Record a 3-sentence description of your dream home: state the total number of bedrooms using 'There are...', describe the kitchen or living room using 'There is a...', and mention an outdoor feature (garden/balcony).
- **HI**: अपने सपनों के घर के बारे में 3 वाक्यों का ऑडियो रिकॉर्ड करें: 'There are...' से कमरों की संख्या बताएं, 'There is a...' से रसोई या बैठक का वर्णन करें, और एक बगीचे या बालकनी का उल्लेख करें।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day16_assess_001]` **Why is 'There have three rooms in my house' incorrect?** ➔ *Because English expresses spatial existence using 'There are' (with the BE verb), not the possession verb 'have'.* [Skill: Syntax Precision]
- `[day16_assess_002]` **When do we use 'There is' vs. 'There are'?** ➔ *'There is' is used before singular count nouns and uncountable nouns; 'There are' is used before plural count nouns.* [Skill: Subject-Verb Agreement]
- `[day16_assess_003]` **How do you form a quantity question about items in a house?** ➔ *Using 'How many + [Plural Noun] + are there + [in Location]?' (e.g., 'How many rooms are there?').* [Skill: Interrogative Mechanics]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `ROOMS_HOUSE_THERE_IS_THERE_ARE_L1`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]

## Day 17: Furniture, Household Objects & Prepositions of Location (In, On, Under, Next to, Between, Behind) {#day-17-day_17}
**शीर्षक**: फ़र्नीचर, घरेलू वस्तुएं और स्थान सूचक संबंधबोधक (In, On, Under, Next to, Behind, Between)  
**CEFR Level**: `A1.1` | **Est. Duration**: 30 mins | **Prerequisites**: Day 05 (Present BE), Day 08 (Demonstratives), Day 16 (There is / There are)  

### 1. Learning Objectives (सीखने के लक्ष्य)
- **EN**: Identify core furniture and household objects accurately in English (sofa, wardrobe, desk, shelf, mirror).
  - **HI**: घर के मुख्य फ़र्नीचर और वस्तुओं के नाम अंग्रेज़ी में सही ढंग से जानना (sofa, wardrobe, desk, shelf, mirror)।
- **EN**: Describe the exact spatial arrangement of objects using primary prepositions of location: 'in', 'on', 'under', 'next to', 'between', 'in front of', and 'behind'.
  - **HI**: स्थान दर्शाने वाले Prepositions (in, on, under, next to, between, in front of, behind) का सही प्रयोग करना।
- **EN**: Distinguish surface contact ('on the table') from containment/enclosure ('in the drawer').
  - **HI**: सतह पर छूने ('on the table') और किसी चीज़ के अंदर होने ('in the drawer') का अंतर समझना।
- **EN**: Eliminate common Hindi-English errors such as omitting 'of' (*'in front the door'*) or confusing 'in' and 'on' (*'in the wall'*).
  - **HI**: हिन्दी से सीधे अनुवाद की गलतियाँ जैसे '*in front the door*' ('of' छोड़ देना) या '*in the wall*' को ठीक करना।

### 2. TEACH (सिद्धांत और अवधारणा)
**हिन्दी व्याख्या**:
जब हम यह बताते हैं कि कोई वस्तु कहाँ रखी है (जैसे: 'चाबियाँ मेज़ पर हैं', 'किताब दराज के अंदर है', 'बिल्ली सोफ़े के नीचे है'), तो हम स्थान दर्शाने वाले Prepositions का प्रयोग करते हैं। 1) 'ON' का प्रयोग तब होता है जब कोई चीज़ किसी सतह को छू रही हो (जैसे: 'on the table', 'on the wall')। 2) 'IN' का प्रयोग किसी बंद या घिरी हुई जगह के अंदर के लिए होता है (जैसे: 'in the box', 'in the drawer')। 3) 'UNDER' का मतलब नीचे होता है (जैसे: 'under the bed')। 4) 'NEXT TO' या 'BESIDE' का मतलब ठीक बगल में होता है। 5) 'BETWEEN' दो चीज़ों के बीच के लिए आता है। 6) 'IN FRONT OF' (के सामने) में 'of' लगाना अनिवार्य है (कभी भी '*in front the table*' न कहें)। 7) 'BEHIND' का मतलब के पीछे होता है।

**English Explanation**:
Prepositions of location specify the spatial relationship between an object (the figure) and a reference point (the ground). The two foundational prepositions are 'ON' (denoting physical contact with a 2D surface: 'on the floor', 'on the desk') and 'IN' (denoting inclusion within a 3D enclosed space: 'in the cupboard', 'in the room'). Complex spatial relations include 'UNDER' (directly below), 'NEXT TO' / 'BESIDE' (adjacent adjacency), 'BETWEEN' (positioned in the middle of two distinct boundaries), 'IN FRONT OF' (facing or preceding, requiring 'of'), and 'BEHIND' (at the back of).

#### Core Rules & Nuanced Grammar:
- **Rule 1: Surface Contact (ON) vs. 3D Enclosure (IN)** [Core Spatial Grammar]
  Use 'ON' for surfaces (on the table, on the wall, on the ceiling). Use 'IN' for containers and rooms (in the drawer, in the kitchen, in the box).
- **Rule 2: Mandatory 'OF' in 'In Front Of'** [Universal Grammar Standard]
  The compound preposition 'in front of' must always include 'of'. Correct: 'in front of the TV'. Incorrect: '*in front the TV*'.
- **Rule 3: The Duality Constraint of 'Between'** [Prepositional Accuracy]
  Use 'between' when locating something in the middle of two specific entities ('between the lamp and the sofa').
- **Rule 4: Locative Sentence Construction Patterns** [Syntax Standard]
  Pattern A: [Item] + is/are + [Preposition] + the + [Reference]. Pattern B: There is/are + [Item] + [Preposition] + the + [Reference].

#### Master Formula Table:
```json
[
  {
    "preposition": "ON (Surface)",
    "meaningHi": "पर (सतह के ऊपर)",
    "exampleEn": "The laptop is on the desk.",
    "meaningEnHi": "लैपटॉप मेज़ पर है।"
  },
  {
    "preposition": "IN (Enclosure)",
    "meaningHi": "में / अंदर",
    "exampleEn": "The documents are in the drawer.",
    "meaningEnHi": "दस्तावेज़ दराज़ में हैं।"
  },
  {
    "preposition": "UNDER (Below)",
    "meaningHi": "के नीचे",
    "exampleEn": "The shoes are under the bed.",
    "meaningEnHi": "जूते बिस्तर के नीचे हैं।"
  },
  {
    "preposition": "NEXT TO (Adjacent)",
    "meaningHi": "के बगल में",
    "exampleEn": "The lamp is next to the sofa.",
    "meaningEnHi": "लैंप सोफ़े के बगल में है।"
  },
  {
    "preposition": "IN FRONT OF",
    "meaningHi": "के सामने",
    "exampleEn": "There is a rug in front of the fireplace.",
    "meaningEnHi": "अंगीठी के सामने एक कालीन है।"
  },
  {
    "preposition": "BEHIND (Rear)",
    "meaningHi": "के पीछे",
    "exampleEn": "The broom is behind the door.",
    "meaningEnHi": "झाड़ू दरवाज़े के पीछे है।"
  },
  {
    "preposition": "BETWEEN",
    "meaningHi": "दो के बीच में",
    "exampleEn": "The table is between two armchairs.",
    "meaningEnHi": "मेज़ दो कुर्सियों के बीच में है।"
  }
]
```

#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: Omitting 'of' in 'in front of' (*'in front the mirror'*) and In/On Confusion
- **Hindi Pattern**: हिन्दी में 'मेज़ पर' और 'कमरे में' के आधार पर शिक्षार्थी 'on the room' या 'in the table' जैसी गलतियाँ कर बैठते हैं, और 'in front' के बाद 'of' भूल जाते हैं।
- **Potential Interference**: `Saying '*The car is in front the house*' or '*Put the plate in the table*'.`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: A plate sits on the flat surface (ON the table). A compound preposition requires its particle 'of' (IN FRONT OF).
- **Natural English Solution**: **Always say 'on the table' and 'in front of the house'.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day17_vocab_001` | **Table** | Noun | `/ˈteɪ.bəl/` | मेज़ | The keys are on the dining table. <br> *चाबियाँ खाने की मेज़ पर हैं।* |
| `day17_vocab_002` | **Chair** | Noun | `/tʃeər/` | कुर्सी | Please pull up a chair and sit down. <br> *कृपया एक कुर्सी खींचें और बैठ जाएं।* |
| `day17_vocab_003` | **Sofa** | Noun | `/ˈsəʊ.fə/` | सोफ़ा | There is a three-seater sofa in the living room. <br> *लिविंग रूम में तीन सीटों वाला एक सोफ़ा है।* |
| `day17_vocab_004` | **Bed** | Noun | `/bed/` | बिस्तर / पलंग | There is a comfortable king-size bed in the room. <br> *कमरे में एक आरामदायक बड़ा बिस्तर है।* |
| `day17_vocab_005` | **Wardrobe** | Noun | `/ˈwɔː.drəʊb/` | अलमारी (कपड़ों की) | Hang your coat inside the wardrobe. <br> *अपना कोट अलमारी के अंदर टांग दीजिए।* |
| `day17_vocab_006` | **Desk** | Noun | `/desk/` | लिखने-पढ़ने की मेज़ / डेस्क | My study desk is next to the window. <br> *मेरी पढ़ाई की मेज़ खिड़की के बगल में है।* |
| `day17_vocab_007` | **Shelf** | Noun | `/ʃelf/` | शेल्फ़ / ताक | The books are arranged on the top shelf. <br> *किताबें सबसे ऊपर वाली शेल्फ़ पर रखी हैं।* |
| `day17_vocab_008` | **Mirror** | Noun | `/ˈmɪr.ər/` | आईना / दर्पण | There is a large mirror in front of the sink. <br> *वॉशबेसिन के सामने एक बड़ा आईना है।* |
| `day17_vocab_009` | **Drawer** | Noun | `/drɔːr/` | दराज़ | Your passport is in the top drawer. <br> *आपका पासपोर्ट सबसे ऊपर वाली दराज़ में है।* |
| `day17_vocab_010` | **Lamp** | Noun | `/læmp/` | दीपक / लैंप | Turn on the reading lamp on your desk. <br> *अपनी डेस्क पर लगा पढ़ने वाला लैंप जलाएं।* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day17_phrase_001` [Daily Inquiry]: **"Where are my keys?"** — *मेरी चाबियाँ कहाँ हैं?*
- `day17_phrase_002` [Locative Response]: **"They are on the coffee table."** — *वे कॉफ़ी टेबल पर रखी हैं।*
- `day17_phrase_003` [Precise Location]: **"It is right behind the door."** — *यह ठीक दरवाज़े के पीछे है।*
- `day17_phrase_004` [Direction]: **"Look inside the bottom drawer."** — *सबसे नीचे वाली दराज़ के अंदर देखिए।*
- `day17_phrase_005` [Spatial Description]: **"The desk is between the two windows."** — *डेस्क दो खिड़कियों के बीच में है।*
- `day17_phrase_006` [Instruction]: **"Put it next to the laptop."** — *इसे लैपटॉप के बगल में रख दीजिए।*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day17_example_001`: **My mobile phone is on the bedside table.**  
  *मेरा मोबाइल फ़ोन बिस्तर के पास वाली मेज़ पर है।*
- `day17_example_002`: **The cat is sleeping peacefully under the sofa.**  
  *बिल्ली सोफ़े के नीचे शांति से सो रही है।*
- `day17_example_003`: **There is a tall bookshelf between the door and the wardrobe.**  
  *दरवाज़े और अलमारी के बीच किताबों की एक ऊंची शेल्फ़ है।*
- `day17_example_004`: **Please don't stand right in front of the television.**  
  *कृपया टेलीविज़न के ठीक सामने मत खड़े होइए।*
- `day17_example_005`: **The spare umbrella is behind the main entrance door.**  
  *अतिरिक्त छाता मुख्य प्रवेश द्वार के पीछे है।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Smooth reduction in 'next to' -> /ˈneks.tə/ (silent 't' in 'next'), Weak schwa in 'in front of' -> /ɪn ˈfrʌnt.əv/, Rhotic vowel in 'mirror' /ˈmɪr.ər/ and 'drawer' /drɔːr/
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Do NOT pronounce 'drawer' as 'draw-er' with two distinct syllables; in standard English, it is pronounced as a single syllable /drɔːr/.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day17_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "The keys are on the kitchen counter."
- `day17_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "The charger is in the desk drawer."
- `day17_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "The chair is next to the wardrobe."
- `day17_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "He is standing in front of the mirror."

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (35 Items)
#### 1. `[day17_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PREPOSITIONS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct preposition: 'The coffee cup is ______ the table.'
**Options**: `in` | `on` | `under` | `between`
- **Correct Answer**: `on`
- **Explanation (EN)**: A cup rests on a flat surface, so use 'on the table'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: मेज़ की सतह के ऊपर के लिए 'on the table' आता है।

#### 2. `[day17_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `COMPOUND_PREPOSITIONS`)
**Prompt**: Select the grammatically correct phrase for 'आईने के सामने':
**Options**: `in front the mirror` | `in front of the mirror` | `in front to the mirror` | `front of mirror`
- **Correct Answer**: `in front of the mirror`
- **Explanation (EN)**: The full compound preposition is 'in front of'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही वाक्यांश 'in front of the mirror' है।

#### 3. `[day17_ex_003]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PREPOSITIONS`)
**Prompt**: Choose the correct sentence to say 'मेरी चाबियाँ दराज़ के अंदर हैं':
**Options**: `My keys are on the drawer.` | `My keys are in the drawer.` | `My keys are between the drawer.` | `My keys are at the drawer.`
- **Correct Answer**: `My keys are in the drawer.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: A drawer is an enclosed 3D space, requiring 'in'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: दराज़ के अंदर होने के लिए 'in the drawer' आता है।

#### 4. `[day17_ex_004]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PREPOSITIONS`)
**Prompt**: Complete the sentence: 'The study table is placed ______ the two windows.'
**Options**: `between` | `behind` | `next` | `under`
- **Correct Answer**: `between`
- **Explanation (EN)**: When positioned in the middle of two items, use 'between'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: दो खिड़कियों के बीच के लिए 'between' का प्रयोग करें।

#### 5. `[day17_ex_005]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `PREPOSITIONS`)
**Prompt**: Which sentence is CORRECT?
**Options**: `The cat is sleeping under the bed.` | `The cat is sleeping under of the bed.` | `The cat is sleeping below to the bed.` | `The cat is sleeping beneath of the bed.`
- **Correct Answer**: `The cat is sleeping under the bed.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Under' is a simple preposition directly taking the object 'the bed'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Under the bed' सही रूप है।

#### 6. `[day17_ex_006]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: The painting is hanging ______ (on / in) the living room wall.
- **Correct Answer**: `on`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Surfaces like walls take 'on'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: दीवार की सतह के लिए 'on the wall' आता है।

#### 7. `[day17_ex_007]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: Put your shoes ______ (under / between) the shoe rack, not in the middle of the floor.
- **Correct Answer**: `under`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `on`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'under' or 'on' for positioning near furniture.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Under' या 'on' का प्रयोग करें।

#### 8. `[day17_ex_008]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: There is a small side table ______ (next to / next) the armchair.
- **Correct Answer**: `next to`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Next' requires 'to' when indicating adjacent location: 'next to'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: बगल में दर्शाने के लिए 'next to' पूरा वाक्यांश आता है।

#### 9. `[day17_ex_009]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: The umbrella is standing ______ (behind / between) the front door.
- **Correct Answer**: `behind`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Positioned at the rear of the door: 'behind the front door'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: दरवाज़े के पीछे के लिए 'behind' आएगा।

#### 10. `[day17_ex_010]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: There is a mirror right in front ______ (of / to) the bathroom sink.
- **Correct Answer**: `of`
- **Explanation (EN)**: The formula is 'in front OF'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'In front' के साथ 'of' आता है।

#### 11. `[day17_ex_011]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'The car is parked in front the main gate.'
- **Correct Answer**: `The car is parked in front of the main gate.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Insert 'of' into the compound preposition: 'in front OF the main gate'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'In front' के बाद 'of' लगाना आवश्यक है: 'in front of the main gate'.

#### 12. `[day17_ex_012]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'The books are in the table.'
- **Correct Answer**: `The books are on the table.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Books lie on top of a table's surface (on), not inside its wood (in).
- **Explanation (HI)**: किताबें मेज़ के ऊपर रखी हैं, इसलिए 'on the table' लिखें।

#### 13. `[day17_ex_013]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'The lamp is next the sofa.'
- **Correct Answer**: `The lamp is next to the sofa.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Next' must be followed by 'to': 'next to the sofa'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Next' के साथ 'to' जोड़ें: 'next to the sofa'.

#### 14. `[day17_ex_014]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (easy | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'The clock is hanging in the wall.'
- **Correct Answer**: `The clock is hanging on the wall.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Surfaces take 'on': 'on the wall'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: दीवार पर घड़ी टंगी होने के लिए 'on the wall' आता है।

#### 15. `[day17_ex_015]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'The desk is between of the two windows.'
- **Correct Answer**: `The desk is between the two windows.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Between' does not take 'of'. Use 'between the two windows'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Between' के बाद 'of' नहीं लगता; 'between the two windows' लिखें।

#### 16. `[day17_ex_016]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words to describe an object's location:
- **Correct Answer**: `The laptop is on the desk`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject (The laptop) + is + on the desk.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: The laptop is on the desk.

- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: The pens are in the top drawer.

**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a sentence:
- **Correct Answer**: `The cat is sleeping under the sofa`
- **Explanation (EN)**: The cat + is sleeping + under the sofa.

**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a sentence:

- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: The wardrobe is next to the bed.

**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'मेरी कार की चाबियाँ मेज़ पर हैं।'

- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `The keys to my car are on the table.`, `My car keys are on the desk.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'My car keys are on the table' uses plural agreement and 'on'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'My car keys are on the table.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 22. `[day17_ex_022]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (medium | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'किताबें अलमारी की सबसे ऊपर वाली शेल्फ़ पर हैं।'
- **Correct Answer**: `The books are on the top shelf of the cupboard.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `The books are on the top shelf.`, `The books are on the top shelf of the wardrobe.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'The books are on the top shelf' is natural and precise.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'The books are on the top shelf.' सही अनुवाद है।

- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Don't stand in front of the TV' uses the imperative and 'in front of'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Don't stand in front of the TV.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 24. `[day17_ex_024]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)

- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `There is a lamp between the sofa and the window.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'The lamp is between the sofa and the window' uses 'between [A] and [B]'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'The lamp is between the sofa and the window.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 25. `[day17_ex_025]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'आपका पासपोर्ट सबसे नीचे वाली दराज़ में है।'
- **Correct Answer**: `Your passport is in the bottom drawer.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Your passport is inside the bottom drawer.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Your passport is in the bottom drawer' correctly uses 'in'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Your passport is in the bottom drawer.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 26. `[day17_ex_026]` [TRANSLATION_EN_HI] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into natural Hindi: 'There is a wastebasket next to the study desk.'

- **Correct Answer**: `The [object 1] is [preposition] the [item], and the [object 2] is [preposition] the [item].`

**Prompt**: Why is 'The painting is in the wall' incorrect?
- **Correct Answer**: `Because a painting hangs on the outer surface of a wall, so 'on the wall' is required.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'on' for flat 2D surfaces.
- **Explanation (HI)**: दीवार की सतह पर होने के कारण 'on the wall' आता है, 'in' नहीं।

- **Explanation (EN)**: The full compound preposition is 'in front of'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'In front' के बाद 'of' छूटा हुआ है।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: Helping a family member search for mislaid everyday items (keys, glasses, laptop charger) around the home on a busy morning.

#### Dialogue (`day17_dialogue_001`):
- **Sanjay**: "Pooja, have you seen my reading glasses? I can't find them anywhere!"  
  *(पूजा, क्या तुमने मेरा पढ़ने वाला चश्मा देखा है? मुझे वह कहीं नहीं मिल रहा!)*
- **Pooja**: "Did you check on the coffee table in the living room?"  
  *(क्या तुमने लिविंग रूम में कॉफ़ी टेबल पर देखा?)*
- **Sanjay**: "Yes, I looked there. There is only a magazine and a TV remote on the table."  
  *(हाँ, मैंने वहाँ देखा। मेज़ पर केवल एक पत्रिका और टीवी का रिमोट है।)*
- **Pooja**: "Look inside the top drawer of the bedside table in the bedroom."  
  *(बेडरूम में बेड के पास वाली मेज़ की सबसे ऊपर वाली दराज़ के अंदर देखो।)*
- **Sanjay**: "Ah, here they are! They were right behind my diary. Thank you!"  
  *(अरे, ये रहे! ये मेरी डायरी के ठीक पीछे थे। धन्यवाद!)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Record a 3-sentence description directing someone to find a missing item in your home: specify the room, the piece of furniture, and the exact prepositional location (e.g. 'on the desk, right next to the lamp').
- **HI**: किसी को घर में कोई वस्तु ढूंढने के निर्देश देने वाला 3 वाक्यों का ऑडियो रिकॉर्ड करें: कमरा, फ़र्नीचर और सटीक Preposition (जैसे 'on the desk, next to the lamp') बताएं।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day17_assess_001]` **What is the key geometric difference between using 'on' and 'in' for household objects?** ➔ *'On' denotes contact with a flat 2D surface (table, wall, floor); 'in' denotes containment within a 3D enclosure (drawer, cupboard, box).* [Skill: Spatial Precision]
- `[day17_assess_002]` **Why is 'The car is in front the house' ungrammatical?** ➔ *Because the compound preposition requires the relational particle 'of' ('in front OF the house').* [Skill: Syntax Precision]
- `[day17_assess_003]` **How do you pronounce the noun 'drawer' in standard English?** ➔ *As a single rhotic syllable /drɔːr/, never as a two-syllable word like 'draw-er'.* [Skill: Phonological Precision]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `FURNITURE_PREPOSITIONS_LOCATION_L1`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 3, 7, 14]
