#### ⚠️ Hindi Speaker Trap & Contrastive Solution:
- **Trap**: The Stage 2 Hall of Fame Trap Breakdown (9 Traps)
- **Hindi Pattern**: स्टेज 2 के 9 सबसे घातक हिन्दी-अंग्रेज़ी हस्तक्षेप:
- **Potential Interference**: `1. *doesn't likes* -> doesn't like
2. *how much rupees* -> how much does it cost / how many rupees
3. *like to playing* -> like playing / like to play
4. *can to swim* -> can swim
5. *there have three rooms* -> there are three rooms
6. *in front the door* -> in front OF the door
7. *in night* -> at night
8. *by walk* -> on foot / walk
9. *by my car* -> in my car / by car`
- **Linguistic Explanation**: Each of these 9 errors stems from a direct 1-to-1 word substitution from Hindi grammatical habits into English syntax.
- **Natural English Solution**: **Commit the standard English patterns to muscle memory through active vocalization.**

### 3. LEARN (शब्दावली और वाक्यांश)
#### Core Vocabulary (10 words):
| ID | Word | POS | IPA | Hindi Meaning | Example (EN / HI) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `day20_vocab_001` | **Commute** | Verb/Noun | `/kəˈmjuːt/` | रोज़ की यात्रा | How long does your commute take? <br> *आपकी रोज़ की यात्रा में कितना समय लगता है?* |
| `day20_vocab_002` | **Convenient** | Adjective | `/kənˈviː.ni.ənt/` | सुविधाजनक | Traveling by metro is very convenient. <br> *मेट्रो से यात्रा करना बहुत सुविधाजनक है।* |
| `day20_vocab_003` | **Routine** | Noun | `/ruːˈtiːn/` | दिनचर्या | I follow a strict morning routine. <br> *मैं सुबह की एक सख्त दिनचर्या का पालन करता हूँ।* |
| `day20_vocab_004` | **Spacious** | Adjective | `/ˈspeɪ.ʃəs/` | विशाल / खुला-खुला | There is a spacious living room in this apartment. <br> *इस अपार्टमेंट में एक बड़ा खुला लिविंग रूम है।* |
| `day20_vocab_005` | **Neighborhood** | Noun | `/ˈneɪ.bə.hʊd/` | आस-पड़ोस / इलाका | Are there any grocery shops in your neighborhood? <br> *क्या आपके पड़ोस में कोई किराने की दुकानें हैं?* |
| `day20_vocab_006` | **Allergic** | Adjective | `/əˈlɜː.dʒɪk/` | एलर्जी होना | I can't eat peanuts because I am allergic. <br> *मैं मूंगफली नहीं खा सकता क्योंकि मुझे एलर्जी है।* |
| `day20_vocab_007` | **Fluently** | Adverb | `/ˈfluː.ənt.li/` | धाराप्रवाह / बिना अटके | She can speak three languages fluently. <br> *वह तीन भाषाएँ धाराप्रवाह बोल सकती है।* |
| `day20_vocab_008` | **Usually** | Adverb | `/ˈjuː.ʒu.ə.li/` | सामान्यतः / आमतौर पर | We usually have dinner at 8:30 PM. <br> *हम आमतौर पर रात 8:30 बजे खाना खाते हैं।* |
| `day20_vocab_009` | **Vegetarian** | Noun/Adjective | `/ˌvedʒ.ɪˈteə.ri.ən/` | शाकाहारी | Is there any vegetarian food on the menu? <br> *क्या मेन्यू में कोई शाकाहारी भोजन है?* |
| `day20_vocab_010` | **Available** | Adjective | `/əˈveɪ.lə.bəl/` | उपलब्ध | Are there any tickets available for the evening show? <br> *क्या शाम के शो के लिए कोई टिकट उपलब्ध हैं?* |

#### Conversational Phrases & Expressions:
- `day20_phrase_001` [Polite Request]: **"Could you please pass me the salt?"** — *क्या आप कृपया मुझे नमक पकड़ा देंगे?*
- `day20_phrase_002` [Transit Inquiry]: **"How long does it take to get there?"** — *वहाँ पहुँचने में कितना समय लगता है?*
- `day20_phrase_003` [Routine Preference]: **"I don't eat spicy food at night."** — *मैं रात को मसालेदार खाना नहीं खाता हूँ।*
- `day20_phrase_004` [Description]: **"There is plenty of space in the wardrobe."** — *अलमारी में बहुत सारी जगह है।*
- `day20_phrase_005` [Hobby Expression]: **"I really enjoy listening to classical music."** — *मुझे शास्त्रीय संगीत सुनना बहुत पसंद है।*
- `day20_phrase_006` [Sequenced Routine]: **"First, I exercise, and then I have breakfast."** — *पहले मैं व्यायाम करता हूँ, और फिर नाश्ता करता हूँ।*

### 4. EXAMPLES (मॉडल वाक्य)
- `day20_example_001`: **Rohan doesn't drink milk, but he loves drinking fresh orange juice in the morning.**  
  *रोहन दूध नहीं पीता है, लेकिन उसे सुबह ताज़ा संतरे का जूस पीना बहुत पसंद है।*
- `day20_example_002`: **Do you have any sugar left in the kitchen cabinet? — No, there isn't any.**  
  *क्या रसोई की कैबिनेट में कुछ चीनी बची है? — नहीं, बिल्कुल नहीं है।*
- `day20_example_003`: **Can she drive a car? — No, but she can ride a bicycle very well.**  
  *क्या वह कार चला सकती है? — नहीं, लेकिन वह बहुत अच्छी साइकिल चला सकती है।*
- `day20_example_004`: **There are two laptops on the desk right next to the window.**  
  *खिड़की के ठीक बगल में मेज़ पर दो लैपटॉप रखे हैं।*
- `day20_example_005`: **I always commute to work by metro, but on weekends I go everywhere on foot.**  
  *मैं काम पर हमेशा मेट्रो से जाता हूँ, लेकिन सप्ताहांत पर मैं हर जगह पैदल जाता हूँ।*

### 5. PRONUNCIATION & PHONETICS (उच्चारण और ध्वनि)
- **Target Sounds**: Rapid natural contractions: 'doesn't' /ˈdʌz.ənt/, 'can't' /kɑːnt/, 'there's' /ðeərz/, Voiced dental fricative /ð/ in 'there', 'then', 'that', Polite intonation rises in questions: 'Do you...?', 'Could you please...?'
- **L1 Pronunciation Traps**: Review all pronunciation pitfalls: pronounce 'breakfast' as /ˈbrek.fəst/, 'drawer' as /drɔːr/, 'comfortable' as /ˈkʌmf.tə.bəl/, and 'vegetable' as /ˈvedʒ.tə.bəl/.
#### Audio Drills:
- `day20_audio_001` [TEACHER]: "He doesn't like spicy food, but he enjoys cooking Italian dishes."
- `day20_audio_002` [TEACHER]: "Could you please tell me how long it takes to reach the station?"
- `day20_audio_003` [TEACHER]: "There are three chairs in front of the large mirror."
- `day20_audio_004` [TEACHER]: "I usually wake up at six and commute by train on weekdays."

### 6. PRACTICE EXERCISES (45 Items)
#### 1. `[day20_ex_001]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `STAGE2_SYNTHESIS`)
**Prompt**: STAGE 2 CUMULATIVE: Which sentence is 100% grammatically correct?
**Options**: `She doesn't likes tea, but she like to drinking coffee.` | `She doesn't like tea, but she likes drinking coffee.` | `She don't like tea, but she like drinking coffee.` | `She doesn't likes tea, but she likes to drinking coffee.`
- **Correct Answer**: `She doesn't like tea, but she likes drinking coffee.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: After 'doesn't', use base form 'like'. In affirmative 3rd person, use 'likes drinking' (gerund complement).
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Doesn't' के बाद base form 'like' आता है, और सकारात्मक में 'likes drinking' सही है।

#### 2. `[day20_ex_002]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `STAGE2_SYNTHESIS`)
**Prompt**: STAGE 2 CUMULATIVE: Choose the correct sentence to describe transport and routine:
**Options**: `I go to office daily by walk at 9:00 AM.` | `I go to the office daily on foot at 9:00 AM.` | `I go to office daily by foot on 9:00 AM.` | `I go to the office daily in walk in 9:00 AM.`
- **Correct Answer**: `I go to the office daily on foot at 9:00 AM.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'on foot' for walking and 'at' for clock times.
- **Explanation (HI)**: पैदल के लिए 'on foot' और समय के लिए 'at 9:00 AM' का प्रयोग होता है।

#### 3. `[day20_ex_003]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `STAGE2_SYNTHESIS`)
**Prompt**: STAGE 2 CUMULATIVE: Choose the correct restaurant request:
**Options**: `Could you please to bring some water?` | `Could you please bring some water?` | `Can you please to bringing some water?` | `Could you bringing some water please?`
- **Correct Answer**: `Could you please bring some water?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Modal 'Could you please' is followed directly by base verb 'bring' (no 'to').
- **Explanation (HI)**: विनम्र निवेदन में 'Could you please bring...' आता है ('to' नहीं लगता)।

#### 4. `[day20_ex_004]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `STAGE2_SYNTHESIS`)
**Prompt**: STAGE 2 CUMULATIVE: Select the correct apartment description:
**Options**: `There have three rooms and a garden in front the house.` | `There are three rooms and a garden in front of the house.` | `It is three rooms and a garden in front of the house.` | `There is three rooms and a garden in front the house.`
- **Correct Answer**: `There are three rooms and a garden in front of the house.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Plural existence takes 'There are', and compound preposition is 'in front of'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'There are three rooms' और 'in front OF the house' दोनों नियम एक साथ सही हैं।

#### 5. `[day20_ex_005]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `STAGE2_SYNTHESIS`)
**Prompt**: STAGE 2 CUMULATIVE: Choose the correct sentence with quantifiers:
**Options**: `How many water do you drink every day?` | `How much glasses of water do you drink every day?` | `How many glasses of water do you drink every day?` | `How much water glasses do you drink every day?`
- **Correct Answer**: `How many glasses of water do you drink every day?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Glasses' is countable plural, requiring 'How many'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: गिलास (glasses) गणनीय बहुवचन है, इसलिए 'How many glasses' आएगा।

#### 6. `[day20_ex_006]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: Ramesh ______ (doesn't have / doesn't has) any brothers or sisters.
- **Correct Answer**: `doesn't have`
- **Explanation (EN)**: After 'doesn't', use base form 'have', never 'has'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Doesn't' के बाद हमेशा base form 'have' आता है।

#### 7. `[day20_ex_007]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: Do you ______ (enjoy playing / enjoy to play) chess in the evening?
- **Correct Answer**: `enjoy playing`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Enjoy' is strictly followed by a gerund (-ing).
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Enjoy' के बाद हमेशा verb-ing आता है: 'enjoy playing'.

#### 8. `[day20_ex_008]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: He can ______ (swim / to swim) across the river easily.
- **Correct Answer**: `swim`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Modal 'can' takes bare infinitive 'swim' without 'to'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Can' के बाद 'to' नहीं लगता, केवल 'swim' आता है।

#### 9. `[day20_ex_009]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: There ______ (is / are) some hot soup in the bowl on the table.
- **Correct Answer**: `is`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Soup' is uncountable, requiring 'There is'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Soup' अगणनीय है, इसलिए 'There is' आएगा।

#### 10. `[day20_ex_010]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: We usually watch a movie ______ (at / in) night on Saturdays.
- **Correct Answer**: `at`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Night takes 'at night'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: रात के लिए 'at night' आता है।

#### 11. `[day20_ex_011]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: How ______ (long / much) does it take to commute to your office by metro?
- **Correct Answer**: `long`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'How long does it take' asks about travel time.
- **Explanation (HI)**: समय पूछने के लिए 'How long does it take' आता है।

#### 12. `[day20_ex_012]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: The keys are inside the drawer, right ______ (under / between) the diary and the pen.
- **Correct Answer**: `between`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Between two objects (diary and pen).
- **Explanation (HI)**: दो वस्तुओं के बीच के लिए 'between' आता है।

#### 13. `[day20_ex_013]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (hard | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'My brother doesn't plays cricket because he likes to playing football.'
- **Correct Answer**: `My brother doesn't play cricket because he likes playing football.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `My brother doesn't play cricket because he likes to play football.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Change 'doesn't plays' to 'doesn't play' and 'likes to playing' to 'likes playing' or 'likes to play'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'doesn't play' और 'likes playing' (या likes to play) लिखें।

#### 14. `[day20_ex_014]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'There have two cars parked in front the gate.'
- **Correct Answer**: `There are two cars parked in front of the gate.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Replace 'There have' with 'There are' and add 'of' to 'in front of'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'There have' की जगह 'There are' और 'in front of' लिखें।

#### 15. `[day20_ex_015]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (hard | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'I come to college daily by walk at 8:00 AM in morning.'
- **Correct Answer**: `I come to college daily on foot at 8:00 AM in the morning.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `I walk to college daily at 8:00 AM in the morning.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Change 'by walk' to 'on foot' (or 'I walk') and 'in morning' to 'in THE morning'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'On foot' और 'in the morning' सही रूप हैं।

#### 16. `[day20_ex_016]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (hard | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'Can you to help me find how much apples are in the basket?'
- **Correct Answer**: `Can you help me find how many apples are in the basket?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Remove 'to' after modal 'can', and change 'how much apples' to 'how many apples'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Can you help' ('to' हटाएं) और 'how many apples' लिखें।

#### 17. `[day20_ex_017]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (hard | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'We traveled to Delhi by my car on 6:00 AM in night.'
- **Correct Answer**: `We traveled to Delhi in my car at 6:00 AM at night.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `We traveled to Delhi in my car at 6:00 AM.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'In my car' (with possessive), 'at 6:00 AM' (clock time), 'at night'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'In my car', 'at 6:00 AM' और 'at night' सही Prepositions हैं।

#### 18. `[day20_ex_018]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a correct complex sentence:
- **Correct Answer**: `He doesn't drink coffee in the morning`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Subject + doesn't + drink + coffee + in the morning.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: He doesn't drink coffee in the morning.

#### 19. `[day20_ex_019]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a polite question:
- **Correct Answer**: `Could you please pass me the salt`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Could + you + please + pass me the salt?
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: Could you please pass me the salt?

#### 20. `[day20_ex_020]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a descriptive sentence:
- **Correct Answer**: `There is a large sofa in the living room`
- **Explanation (EN)**: There is + a large sofa + in the living room.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: There is a large sofa in the living room.

#### 21. `[day20_ex_021]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a transit sentence:
- **Correct Answer**: `It takes twenty minutes by metro`
- **Explanation (EN)**: It + takes + twenty minutes + by metro.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: It takes twenty minutes by metro.

#### 22. `[day20_ex_022]` [SENTENCE_ORDERING] (easy | `SENTENCE_ORDER`)
**Prompt**: Arrange the words into a sequenced routine:
- **Correct Answer**: `First I wake up at 6:00`
- **Explanation (EN)**: First + I + wake up + at 6:00.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सही क्रम: First I wake up at 6:00.

#### 23. `[day20_ex_023]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (medium | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'वह रात में चाय नहीं पीता है क्योंकि उसे नींद नहीं आती।'
- **Correct Answer**: `He doesn't drink tea at night because he can't sleep.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `He does not drink tea at night because he cannot sleep.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'He doesn't drink tea at night because he can't sleep' combines negation, time preposition, and ability modal.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'He doesn't drink tea at night because he can't sleep.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 24. `[day20_ex_024]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (medium | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'क्या आपके पास कोई अतिरिक्त कुर्सियाँ हैं? — नहीं, यहाँ कोई नहीं है।'
- **Correct Answer**: `Do you have any extra chairs? — No, there aren't any.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `Do you have any spare chairs? — No, there aren't any.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Uses 'any extra chairs' in question and 'there aren't any' in negative existential response.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Do you have any extra chairs? — No, there aren't any.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 25. `[day20_ex_025]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (medium | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'मुझे सप्ताहांत पर गिटार बजाना और गाने सुनना बहुत पसंद है।'
- **Correct Answer**: `I love playing the guitar and listening to songs on weekends.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `I really enjoy playing the guitar and listening to music on weekends.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Uses 'love playing... and listening' (parallel gerunds) and 'on weekends'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'I love playing the guitar and listening to songs on weekends.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 26. `[day20_ex_026]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (medium | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'दफ़्तर पहुँचने में कितना समय लगता है? — मेट्रो से लगभग तीस मिनट लगते हैं।'
- **Correct Answer**: `How long does it take to get to the office? — It takes about thirty minutes by metro.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `How long does it take to reach the office? — It takes about 30 minutes by metro.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'How long does it take...' paired with 'It takes about thirty minutes by metro'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'How long does it take to get to the office? — It takes about thirty minutes by metro.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 27. `[day20_ex_027]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (hard | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'आईने के सामने एक छोटी मेज़ है, और चाबियाँ दराज़ में हैं।'
- **Correct Answer**: `There is a small table in front of the mirror, and the keys are in the drawer.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `There is a small desk in front of the mirror, and the keys are in the drawer.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Synthesizes 'There is', 'in front of the mirror', and 'in the drawer'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'There is a small table in front of the mirror, and the keys are in the drawer.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 28. `[day20_ex_028]` [TRANSLATION_EN_HI] (easy | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into natural Hindi: 'Could you please tell me where the nearest metro station is?'
- **Correct Answer**: `क्या आप कृपया मुझे बता सकते हैं कि सबसे नजदीकी मेट्रो स्टेशन कहाँ है?`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `क्या आप बता सकते हैं कि सबसे पास का मेट्रो स्टेशन कहाँ है?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Could you please tell me...' translates to polite Hindi 'क्या आप कृपया मुझे बता सकते हैं...'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'क्या आप कृपया मुझे बता सकते हैं कि सबसे नजदीकी मेट्रो स्टेशन कहाँ है?' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 29. `[day20_ex_029]` [TRANSLATION_EN_HI] (medium | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into natural Hindi: 'I always commute on foot because my office is next to my house.'
- **Correct Answer**: `मैं हमेशा पैदल आता-जाता हूँ क्योंकि मेरा दफ़्तर मेरे घर के बगल में है।`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `मैं हमेशा पैदल जाता हूँ क्योंकि मेरा ऑफिस मेरे घर के पास है।`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'Commute on foot' translates to 'पैदल आना-जाना' and 'next to' translates to 'के बगल में'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'मैं हमेशा पैदल आता-जाता हूँ क्योंकि मेरा दफ़्तर मेरे घर के बगल में है।' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 30. `[day20_ex_030]` [LISTENING_DICTATION] (hard | `LISTENING`)
**Prompt**: Listen to the audio and write down the complete synthesized sentence:
- **Correct Answer**: `She doesn't like driving in heavy traffic, so she usually takes the metro at eight thirty.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `She doesn't like driving in heavy traffic, so she usually takes the metro at 8:30.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Synthesizes negation, gerund complement, frequency adverb, transit mode, and clock time.
- **Explanation (HI)**: वाक्य को ध्यान से सुनकर लिखें।

#### 31. `[day20_ex_031]` [LISTENING_DICTATION] (medium | `LISTENING`)
**Prompt**: Listen and write down the multi-clause sentence:
- **Correct Answer**: `There are two bedrooms upstairs, and the bathroom is right next to the master bedroom.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `There are 2 bedrooms upstairs, and the bathroom is right next to the master bedroom.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Synthesizes existential 'There are' and locative 'next to'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: ऑडियो को ध्यान से सुनकर पूरा वाक्य लिखें।

#### 32. `[day20_ex_032]` [MATCHING] (easy | `MATCHING`)
**Prompt**: STAGE 2 MASTER MATCH: Match the incorrect Indian English phrase with its correct global English standard:
- **Correct Answer**: `Matched correctly`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Match each common interference error to its authentic English counterpart.
- **Explanation (HI)**: प्रत्येक सामान्य गलती का उसके सही अंग्रेज़ी रूप से मिलान करें।

#### 33. `[day20_ex_033]` [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (medium | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the real-life synthesis dialogue: Host: 'Would you like some tea or coffee?' Guest: 'I ______ (not / drink) coffee in the evening, but I would love ______ (a / some) herbal tea, please.'
- **Correct Answer**: `don't drink, some`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `do not drink, some`, `don't drink / some`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Negative routine: 'don't drink'. Polite offer acceptance of uncountable: 'some herbal tea'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Don't drink' और 'some herbal tea' सही उत्तर हैं।

#### 34. `[day20_ex_034]` [DIALOGUE_COMPLETION] (medium | `CONVERSATIONAL_USE`)
**Prompt**: Complete the travel inquiry: Traveler: 'Excuse me, ______ (can / you / tell) me how long it ______ (take) to get to Connaught Place by metro?' Clerk: 'It takes about fifteen minutes.'
- **Correct Answer**: `can you tell, takes`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `could you tell, takes`, `can you tell / takes`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Modal request: 'can/could you tell me'. Duration verb: 'it takes'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Can you tell' और 'takes' सही उत्तर हैं।

#### 35. `[day20_ex_035]` [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (medium | `SPEAKING`)
**Prompt**: Speak aloud: In 3 full sentences, state: (1) what time you wake up, (2) how you travel to work/college, and (3) one hobby you enjoy in the evening.
- **Correct Answer**: `I wake up at [time]. I go to [work/college] by [vehicle/on foot]. I enjoy [hobby+ing] in the evening.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Integrate time prepositions, transit idioms, and gerund hobby expressions seamlessly.
- **Explanation (HI)**: समय, साधन और शौक को जोड़ते हुए 3 पूर्ण वाक्य अंग्रेज़ी में बोलें।

#### 36. `[day20_ex_036]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: Why can you never say 'He doesn't likes coffee'?
- **Correct Answer**: `Because the auxiliary 'doesn't' already carries the 3rd person singular marker; the main verb must remain in its base form 'like'.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Auxiliary 'does' carries the tense/agreement; main verb remains V1.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Doesn't' में पहले से 'es' लगा है, इसलिए मुख्य क्रिया की मूल फ़ॉर्म 'like' ही आती है।

#### 37. `[day20_ex_037]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: State the correct preposition for: (1) 7:00 PM, (2) Monday, (3) the afternoon, (4) night.
- **Correct Answer**: `(1) at, (2) on, (3) in, (4) at`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `at, on, in, at`, `at 7:00 PM, on Monday, in the afternoon, at night`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Clock times (at), Days (on), Day parts (in the), Night (at).
- **Explanation (HI)**: समय (at), दिन (on), दिन का हिस्सा (in the), रात (at)।

#### 38. `[day20_ex_038]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (easy | `STAGE2_SYNTHESIS`)
**Prompt**: STAGE 2 CUMULATIVE: Which question is grammatically CORRECT to ask about someone's hobby?
**Options**: `What do you like to doing in your free time?` | `What do you like doing in your free time?` | `What does you like doing in your free time?` | `What do you liking to do in your free time?`
- **Correct Answer**: `What do you like doing in your free time?`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'What do you like doing...' is proper Present Simple + Gerund complement.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'What do you like doing in your free time?' सही रूप है।

#### 39. `[day20_ex_039]` [MULTIPLE_CHOICE] (medium | `STAGE2_SYNTHESIS`)
**Prompt**: STAGE 2 CUMULATIVE: How do you properly refuse extra food at a dinner party?
**Options**: `No thanks, I can not to eat more.` | `No thank you, I am full and I don't want any more rice.` | `No thanks, I don't want some more rice.` | `No thank you, I am not eating much rice in night.`
- **Correct Answer**: `No thank you, I am full and I don't want any more rice.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Polite decline with 'any more' in negative clause.
- **Explanation (HI)**: नकारात्मक वाक्य में 'any more rice' सही प्रयोग है।

#### 40. `[day20_ex_040]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: Is there ______ (any / some) milk in the fridge, or should I buy a bottle on my way home?
- **Correct Answer**: `any`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Use 'any' in standard information questions about existence.
- **Explanation (HI)**: सामान्य प्रश्न में 'any' का प्रयोग करें।

#### 41. `[day20_ex_041]` [FILL_BLANK] (easy | `FILL_BLANK`)
**Prompt**: She ______ (can / cans) speak both Hindi and English very fluently.
- **Correct Answer**: `can`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Modal verbs like 'can' never take an '-s' ending in 3rd person singular.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Can' में कभी भी 's' नहीं लगता (ही/शी के साथ भी 'can' ही रहता है)।

#### 42. `[day20_ex_042]` [ERROR_CORRECTION] (medium | `ERROR_CORRECTION`)
**Prompt**: Identify and correct the error: 'The doctor is sitting on the room next the reception.'
- **Correct Answer**: `The doctor is sitting in the room next to the reception.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Rooms take 'in', and 'next' requires 'to': 'in the room next TO the reception'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: कमरे के अंदर के लिए 'in' और बगल के लिए 'next to' आता है।

#### 43. `[day20_ex_043]` [TRANSLATION_HI_EN] (hard | `TRANSLATION`)
**Prompt**: Translate into English: 'क्या मैं आपकी कार में बैठ सकता हूँ? — हाँ, बिल्कुल, आगे की सीट पर बैठ जाइए।'
- **Correct Answer**: `Can I sit in your car? — Yes, of course, please sit in the front seat.`
- **Acceptable Alternatives**: `May I sit in your car? — Yes, certainly, sit in the front seat.`, `Can I sit in your car? — Yes, sure, please sit in the front seat.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: 'In your car' (enclosed private vehicle) and 'in the front seat'.
- **Explanation (HI)**: 'Can I sit in your car? — Yes, of course, please sit in the front seat.' सही अनुवाद है।

#### 44. `[day20_ex_044]` [SPOKEN_RESPONSE] (hard | `SPEAKING`)
**Prompt**: Speak aloud: Introduce your home, your family's favorite hobby, and your commute in 3 connected sentences.
- **Correct Answer**: `There are [rooms] in my house. My family loves [hobby+ing]. I commute by [transport].`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Synthesize all Stage 2 communication pillars in a single spoken stream.
- **Explanation (HI)**: घर, परिवार के शौक और अपने आने-जाने के साधन के बारे में 3 वाक्य बोलें।

#### 45. `[day20_ex_045]` [RECALL] (easy | `RECALL`)
**Prompt**: What is the single most important rule when using modals (can, could) and auxiliaries (do, does, don't, doesn't)?
- **Correct Answer**: `They must always be followed directly by the base form of the main verb (V1) without -s/-es, -ing, or 'to'.`
- **Explanation (EN)**: Modals and do-auxiliaries govern the bare infinitive.
- **Explanation (HI)**: इनके बाद हमेशा क्रिया की मूल पहली फ़ॉर्म (V1) आती है (बिना s, ing या to के)।

### 7. USE (प्रासंगिक उपयोग और संवाद)
**Situation**: A comprehensive social conversation at an international community meetup in Bangalore where members introduce their lifestyles, housing, dietary habits, hobbies, and commute routes.

#### Dialogue (`day20_dialogue_001`):
- **Aditi**: "Hello Vikram! Welcome to our weekend meetup. Do you live near Indiranagar?"  
  *(नमस्ते विक्रम! हमारे वीकेंड मीटअप में आपका स्वागत है। क्या आप इंदिरानगर के पास रहते हैं?)*
- **Vikram**: "Hi Aditi! No, I don't live in Indiranagar. I live in Whitefield with my family."  
  *(नमस्ते अदिति! नहीं, मैं इंदिरानगर में नहीं रहता। मैं अपने परिवार के साथ व्हाइटफ़ील्ड में रहता हूँ।)*
- **Aditi**: "Oh, how do you usually get to the city center from Whitefield? How long does it take?"  
  *(ओह, आप आमतौर पर व्हाइटफ़ील्ड से शहर के केंद्र में कैसे आते हैं? इसमें कितना समय लगता है?)*
- **Vikram**: "I always travel by metro because there is too much traffic on the roads. It takes about forty minutes."  
  *(मैं हमेशा मेट्रो से यात्रा करता हूँ क्योंकि सड़कों पर बहुत ट्रैफ़िक होता है। इसमें लगभग चालीस मिनट लगते हैं।)*
- **Aditi**: "That's very convenient! And what do you enjoy doing during your free time on weekends?"  
  *(यह तो बहुत सुविधाजनक है! और आप सप्ताहांत पर अपने खाली समय में क्या करना पसंद करते हैं?)*
- **Vikram**: "I love cooking South Indian food and playing cricket in the morning. Could you please recommend a good vegetarian cafe around here?"  
  *(मुझे दक्षिण भारतीय खाना बनाना और सुबह क्रिकेट खेलना बहुत पसंद है। क्या आप कृपया यहाँ पास में कोई अच्छा शाकाहारी कैफ़े बता सकती हैं?)*
- **Aditi**: "Certainly! There is a wonderful cafe right next to the metro station. Let's go there together on foot!"  
  *(बिल्कुल! मेट्रो स्टेशन के ठीक बगल में एक बहुत बढ़िया कैफ़े है। चलिए वहाँ मिलकर पैदल चलते हैं!)*

#### Free Speaking Production Task:
- **EN**: Record a 6-sentence comprehensive personal introduction: (1) state where you live and describe your home using 'There is/are', (2) describe your work/study commute and how long it takes, (3) mention one food habit using 'don't eat / love eating', and (4) explain your favorite weekend hobby.
- **HI**: 6 वाक्यों का एक विस्तृत व्यक्तिगत परिचय ऑडियो रिकॉर्ड करें: (1) बताएं कि आप कहाँ रहते हैं और 'There is/are' से घर का वर्णन करें, (2) बताएं कि आप काम पर कैसे जाते हैं और कितना समय लगता है, (3) 'don't eat / love eating' से अपने खान-पान की आदत बताएं, और (4) अपने सप्ताहांत के पसंदीदा शौक का उल्लेख करें।

### 8. ASSESS & REVIEW (मूल्यांकन और दोहराव)
- `[day20_assess_001]` **Explain the base verb rule governing 'don't', 'doesn't', 'can', and 'could'.** ➔ *All do-auxiliaries and modal verbs require the bare base form of the main verb (V1) without inflection (-s/-es/-ed/-ing) and without the particle 'to'.* [Skill: Auxiliary Mastery]
- `[day20_assess_002]` **Summarize the 3 core transit rules for 'by', 'in', and 'on'.** ➔ *'By + bare vehicle' denotes general transit without articles (by bus); 'in + a/my' denotes enclosed private vehicles (in my car); 'on + the' denotes public transit platforms (on the train) and pedestrian travel is strictly 'on foot'.* [Skill: Transit Mastery]
- `[day20_assess_003]` **What are the exact time preposition rules for clock times, days, day parts, and night?** ➔ *Clock times take 'at' (at 6:00), specific days take 'on' (on Monday), parts of the day take 'in the' (in the morning), and nighttime habit takes 'at night'.* [Skill: Temporal Mastery]

**Spaced Repetition Tag**: `STAGE2_CUMULATIVE_SYNTHESIS_MASTERY`  
**Recommended Review Intervals**: Day +[1, 7, 14, 30, 60]
